Author: coderabbitai (DB ID: 2971966362)
File: .github/workflows/load-test.yml:17
Body:
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Validate `client_count` before exporting `LOAD_TEST_CLIENTS`.**

`client_count` is free-form input, and Line 177 exports it directly. Invalid values (empty, non-numeric, zero/negative) can propagate to scenario parsing and produce invalid load runs.



<details>
<summary>Suggested patch</summary>

```diff
       client_count:
         description: 'Number of concurrent clients'
         required: false
         default: '5'
@@
       - name: Set environment variables
         run: |
@@
-          echo "LOAD_TEST_CLIENTS=${{ inputs.client_count }}" >> $GITHUB_ENV
+          CLIENT_COUNT="${{ inputs.client_count }}"
+          if ! [[ "$CLIENT_COUNT" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]]; then
+            echo "Invalid client_count: $CLIENT_COUNT (must be a positive integer)" >&2
+            exit 1
+          fi
+          echo "LOAD_TEST_CLIENTS=$CLIENT_COUNT" >> $GITHUB_ENV
```
</details>


Also applies to: 166-178

<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>

File: tests/load/src/workloads/file-workload.ts:67
Body:
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Verification failures are currently not reflected in measured error metrics.**

`file not found` and `size mismatch` throw outside `metrics.measure('downloadFile', ...)`, then get swallowed by the outer catch. That keeps the run green (good), but can underreport verification errors in the metrics output.

<details>
<summary>Suggested patch</summary>

```diff
       // Optionally download and verify
       if (verifyDownloads) {
         const folder = client.getFolderTree().get(rootIpnsName);
         const child = folder?.children.find((c) => c.name === fileName);
         const fileIpnsName =
           child && 'fileMetaIpnsName' in child ? child.fileMetaIpnsName : undefined;
-        if (fileIpnsName) {
-          const downloaded = await metrics.measure(
-            'downloadFile',
-            () => client.downloadFromIpns(fileIpnsName, rootFolderKey),
-            size
-          );
-          if (downloaded.length !== data.length) {
-            throw new Error(
-              `[Client ${pc.id}] Size mismatch: uploaded ${data.length}, downloaded ${downloaded.length}`
-            );
-          }
-        } else if (verifyDownloads) {
-          throw new Error(
-            `[Client ${pc.id}] File "${fileName}" not found in folder state after upload`
-          );
-        }
+        await metrics.measure(
+          'downloadFile',
+          async () => {
+            if (!fileIpnsName) {
+              throw new Error(
+                `[Client ${pc.id}] File "${fileName}" not found in folder state after upload`
+              );
+            }
+            const downloaded = await client.downloadFromIpns(fileIpnsName, rootFolderKey);
+            if (downloaded.length !== data.length) {
+              throw new Error(
+                `[Client ${pc.id}] Size mismatch: uploaded ${data.length}, downloaded ${downloaded.length}`
+              );
+            }
+            return downloaded;
+          },
+          size
+        );
       }
```
</details>

**Use HTTP-failing curl flags in readiness checks.**

At Line 139 and Line 145, `curl -s` returns success even for HTTP 500, so the API can be treated as “ready” while unhealthy. Use `-fsS` so only successful HTTP responses pass.



<details>

             fi

             exit 1
           fi

     1→---
     2→created: 2026-03-22T17:34:27.311Z
     3→title: Refactor api-client from singleton to instance-scoped axios
     4→area: api-client
     5→files:
     6→  - packages/api-client/src/instance.ts
     7→  - packages/api-client/orval.config.ts
     8→  - packages/sdk-core/src/ipns/index.ts
     9→  - packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts
    10→  - packages/sdk-core/src/file/index.ts
    11→  - packages/sdk/src/client.ts
    12→  - packages/sdk/src/types.ts
    13→  - apps/web/src/lib/api-config.ts
    14→  - tests/sdk-e2e/src/fixtures/test-harness.ts
    15→  - tests/sdk-e2e/src/fixtures/multi-account.ts
    16→  - tests/load/src/harness/client-pool.ts
    17→---
    18→
    19→## Problem
    20→
    21→`@cipherbox/api-client` uses a module-level singleton (`setApiClientConfig` / `customInstance`) that all orval-generated API functions import. This means every consumer in the process shares one global axios config — there's no way for two `CipherBoxClient` instances to use different tokens simultaneously.
    22→
    23→**Consequences:**
    24→
    25→- Multi-account SDK E2E tests require a `switchTo()` hack that swaps the global singleton before each operation
    26→- Load test client pools can't truly run in parallel — the singleton's `getAccessToken` always returns client[0]'s token for any code path through sdk-core
    27→- Cross-suite test contamination: if a multi-account suite runs before a single-account suite, the singleton may still point to the wrong account's token (caused a real 401 bug during initial SDK E2E testing)
    28→- The desktop app (which may need multiple vault sessions in future) would hit the same limitation
    29→
    30→**Root cause:** Orval generates standalone functions (`ipnsControllerPublishRecord()`, etc.) that import `customInstance` directly. There's no class or DI — the config has to live somewhere global. This was a pragmatic choice during initial extraction from the web app (single-user browser context), not a deliberate architecture decision.
    31→
    32→**Call chain showing the singleton dependency:**
    33→
    34→```text
    35→CipherBoxClient.createFolder()
    36→  → sdk-core.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish()
    37→    → sdk-core.createAndPublishIpnsRecord()
    38→      → api-client.ipnsControllerPublishRecord()    ← generated, imports customInstance
    39→        → customInstance()                           ← reads module-level _config
    40→```
    41→
    42→## Solution
    43→
    44→### Phase 1: Orval config — generate instance-accepting functions
    45→
    46→Configure orval to generate functions that accept an axios instance parameter instead of importing the singleton. Orval supports a `mutator` option that can inject a custom instance. The generated functions would accept `(config, options?)` where `options` can include a custom axios instance.
    47→
    48→### Phase 2: Thread axios instance through SdkContext
    49→
    50→Extend `SdkContext` (in `packages/sdk-core/src/index.ts`) to include an `axiosInstance` (or a factory function). All sdk-core functions already accept `ctx: SdkContext` — they'd pass `ctx.axiosInstance` through to the generated API functions.
    51→
    52→### Phase 3: CipherBoxClient owns its axios instance
    53→
    54→`CipherBoxClient` constructor creates its own axios instance (via `createAxiosInstance()` which already exists in `instance.ts`). It passes this into `SdkContext`, which flows through to all API calls. Each client is fully self-contained.
    55→
    56→### Phase 4: Backward compatibility for web app
    57→
    58→Keep `setApiClientConfig()` and the module-level singleton working for the web app (single-user browser context). The web app calls it once at module load time and never changes it — this pattern is fine. The singleton becomes the "default" instance; instance-scoped usage is opt-in via `SdkContext.axiosInstance`.
    59→
    60→### Phase 5: Simplify test infrastructure
    61→
    62→- Remove `switchTo()` from `multi-account.ts` — each test context's client uses its own instance
    63→- Remove `setApiClientConfig` calls from test harness — each `CipherBoxClient` creates its own
    64→- Load test client pool gets true parallel operation for free
    65→
    66→**Packages touched:**
    67→
    68→- `packages/api-client` — orval config, instance.ts (add instance parameter to customInstance)
    69→- `packages/sdk-core` — SdkContext type, all functions that call generated API functions (~6 files)
    70→- `packages/sdk` — CipherBoxClient constructor (create own axios instance)
    71→- `apps/web` — api-config.ts (no change needed if backward-compatible)
    72→- `tests/sdk-e2e` — test-harness.ts, multi-account.ts (simplify)
    73→- `tests/load` — client-pool.ts (simplify)
    74→
    75→## Additional cleanup enabled by this refactor
    76→
    77→Once the singleton is eliminated, the following workarounds added in PR #318 can be removed:
    78→
    79→- **`testFetch()` helper** (`tests/sdk-e2e/src/fixtures/test-harness.ts`) — raw fetch calls in test suites (vault-lifecycle, share-operations, invite-link) should be replaced with the generated API client functions. The generated client would use the instance-scoped axios automatically, removing the need for manual header injection. The raw fetch calls exist because the original tests were written before the bypass header was added; the generated client is the proper approach.
    80→- **`fetchHeaders()` helper** — same file, only needed because raw fetch doesn't go through the axios instance. Goes away when raw fetch is replaced with generated client.
    81→- **`setApiClientConfig` re-set in error-cases.test.ts** (line 106) — the event emission test re-sets the singleton because multi-account suites overwrite it. With instance-scoped axios, each client owns its config and this workaround is unnecessary.
    82→- **`defaultHeaders` on `ApiClientConfig`** (`packages/api-client/src/instance.ts`) — added to inject the throttle bypass header into the singleton axios instance. Still useful for backward compatibility, but instance-scoped clients would receive it via constructor config instead.
    83→- **`BypassableThrottlerGuard`** (`apps/api/src/common/guards/throttler-bypass.guard.ts`) — this stays regardless of the refactor; it's the proper server-side mechanism for throttle bypass.
    84→
```

> AGENT

## Refactor api-client from singleton to instance-scoped axios

**Area:** api-client
**Created:** 2026-03-22 (today)
**Files:** 11 files across `api-client`, `sdk-core`, `sdk`, `web`, `sdk-e2e`, and `load` packages

### Problem
The `@cipherbox/api-client` uses a module-level singleton (`setApiClientConfig` / `customInstance`) shared by all consumers. This prevents two `CipherBoxClient` instances from using different tokens simultaneously — causing `switchTo()` hacks in tests, cross-suite contamination, and blocking true parallel load testing.

### Solution
5-phase refactor: configure orval to generate instance-accepting functions → thread axios instance through `SdkContext` → have `CipherBoxClient` own its instance → keep backward compat for web app → simplify test infrastructure by removing workarounds.

---

# Roadmap: CipherBox v1.1 IPFS Infrastructure

## Overview

CipherBox v1.1 transforms the platform from "IPFS as a storage backend with database fallbacks" to "IPFS-native with the database serving only auth." The milestone establishes performance baselines before making changes, replaces the unreliable delegated-ipfs.dev dependency with self-hosted IPNS resolution, migrates rootFolderKey to an IPFS vault blob (achieving true zero-knowledge server), adds BYO-IPFS node support for data sovereignty, and completes performance baselines with client-side instrumentation and load testing after all features are stable.

## Milestones

- **v0.1 Staging MVP** - Phases 1-10 (shipped 2026-02-11)
- **v1.0 Production** - Phases 11-17.1 (shipped 2026-03-05)
- **v1.1 IPFS Infrastructure** - Phases 18-22 (in progress)

## Phases

**Phase Numbering:**

- Integer phases (1, 2, 3): Planned milestone work
- Decimal phases (2.1, 2.2): Urgent insertions (marked with INSERTED)

Decimal phases appear between their surrounding integers in numeric order.

- [x] **Phase 18: Performance Instrumentation** - Server-side Prometheus histograms and Kubo metrics scraping to establish baselines before any architectural changes (completed 2026-03-07)
- [x] **Phase 19: IPNS Resolution Improvement** - Replace delegated-ipfs.dev with self-hosted Someguy sidecar for reliable IPNS routing, add latency histograms for resolve/publish operations (completed 2026-03-07)
- [ ] **Phase 20: Vault Migration** - Move rootFolderKey to IPFS vault blob v2 format, making the server store zero crypto material
- [ ] **Phase 21: BYO-IPFS Node Support** - User-configurable IPFS pinning endpoint with dual-pin strategy, Settings UI, and connection testing
- [ ] **Phase 22: Performance Baselines Completion** - Client-side timing instrumentation, end-to-end journey timing, k6 load testing, and capacity documentation

## Phase Details

### Phase 18: Performance Instrumentation

**Goal**: Operators can observe IPFS/IPNS latency and API performance in Prometheus/Grafana before any architectural changes are made
**Depends on**: Nothing (first phase of milestone)
**Requirements**: PERF-01, PERF-02, PERF-03, PERF-04
**Success Criteria** (what must be TRUE):

1. Prometheus exposes duration histograms for IPNS resolve, IPNS publish, IPFS pin, and IPFS cat operations with per-operation labels
2. API endpoint response times are captured at p50/p95/p99 for all critical routes (auth, IPNS resolve/publish, file upload/download, folder CRUD)
3. Kubo node health metrics (peer count, bandwidth, datastore size) are visible in Prometheus via scraped Kubo endpoint
4. TEE republish batch duration histogram captures per-batch timing with success/failure labels

**Plans:** 2/2 plans complete

Plans:

- [x] 18-01-PLAN.md -- Add IPFS/IPNS + TEE duration histograms to MetricsService and instrument service/controller timing
- [x] 18-02-PLAN.md -- Add Kubo scrape to Alloy, extend Grafana dashboard, create baseline benchmark script

### Phase 19: IPNS Resolution Improvement

**Goal**: Users experience reliable, fast IPNS resolution without dependency on external delegated-ipfs.dev service
**Depends on**: Phase 18 (baselines must exist to measure improvement)
**Requirements**: IPNS-01, IPNS-02, IPNS-03, IPNS-04
**Success Criteria** (what must be TRUE):

1. Self-hosted Someguy is deployed alongside Kubo and serves as the IPNS routing provider, with delegated-ipfs.dev fully removed from the resolution path
2. IPNS resolution completes within 2 seconds in the normal case (DB-first with async DHT verification) and degrades gracefully to DB-only when DHT is slow
3. The standalone recovery tool resolves IPNS records via self-hosted Someguy without depending on the CipherBox API or delegated-ipfs.dev
4. No user-visible errors or stale metadata when the DHT is temporarily unreachable (DB fallback serves correct data)
   **Plans**: 2 plans

Plans:

- [x] 19-01-PLAN.md -- Deploy Someguy Docker sidecar, update routing URL in deploy workflow and .env.example
- [x] 19-02-PLAN.md -- Add IPNS resolve/publish latency histograms and instrument IpnsService with timing

### Phase 19.1: Extract core crypto SDK as shared package (INSERTED)

**Goal:** Web app's crypto and file operation logic is extracted into a five-package layered SDK architecture (@cipherbox/crypto, @cipherbox/core, @cipherbox/api-client, @cipherbox/sdk-core, @cipherbox/sdk) enabling load testing, integration testing, and future CLI usage without a browser context
**Requirements**: SDK-01, SDK-02, SDK-03, SDK-04, SDK-05, SDK-06, SDK-07, SDK-08, SDK-09, SDK-10, SDK-11
**Depends on:** Phase 19
**Plans:** 6/6 plans complete

Plans:

- [ ] 19.1-01-PLAN.md -- Split crypto into crypto + core packages with transitional re-exports
- [ ] 19.1-02-PLAN.md -- Expand api-client package with orval generation and configurable instance
- [ ] 19.1-03-PLAN.md -- Create sdk-core with stateless folder/file/IPFS/IPNS operations
- [ ] 19.1-04-PLAN.md -- Create sdk with stateful CipherBoxClient, events, bin, and share operations
- [ ] 19.1-05-PLAN.md -- Rewire web app hooks and stores to use SDK
- [ ] 19.1-06-PLAN.md -- Remove re-exports, update imports, configure Release Please

### Phase 20: Vault Migration

**Goal**: The server stores zero crypto material -- rootFolderKey lives in the IPFS vault blob, making the server a true zero-knowledge relay
**Depends on**: Phase 19 (IPNS must be reliable before making it a login-adjacent dependency)
**Requirements**: VAULT-01, VAULT-02, VAULT-03, VAULT-04, VAULT-05, VAULT-06
**Success Criteria** (what must be TRUE):

1. New users get vault blob v2 format on first login, with rootFolderKey ECIES-wrapped in the blob header and readable without any database round-trip
2. Existing users are lazily migrated to vault blob v2 on their next folder metadata publish, with the DB copy retained as a permanent fallback
3. The standalone recovery tool parses vault blob v2 and extracts rootFolderKey without needing the CipherBox API or database
4. The desktop app (Rust) parses vault blob v2 and uses the embedded rootFolderKey for FUSE mount initialization
5. The encryptedRootIpnsPrivateKey column is deprecated from the vaults table (HKDF derivation is the canonical path)
   **Plans**: TBD

### Phase 21: BYO-IPFS Node Support

**Goal**: Users can configure their own IPFS node for data sovereignty, with files pinned to both CipherBox and their personal node
**Depends on**: Phase 19 (stable IPNS resolution benefits BYO workflows)

```
     1→import axios, {
     2→  type AxiosInstance,
     3→  type AxiosRequestConfig,
     4→  type AxiosError,
     5→  type AxiosProgressEvent,
     6→  type CancelToken,
     7→} from 'axios';
     8→
     9→export type ApiClientConfig = {
    10→  /** Base URL for the CipherBox API (e.g., "http://localhost:3000") */
    11→  baseUrl: string;
    12→  /** Returns a valid access token. Consumer owns refresh logic. */
    13→  getAccessToken: () => Promise<string>;
    14→  /** Called on 401 response to obtain a new access token. If provided, the failed request is retried once with the new token. */
    15→  refreshAccessToken?: () => Promise<string>;
    16→  /** Send cookies cross-origin (needed for HTTP-only refresh token cookie in web). */
    17→  withCredentials?: boolean;
    18→  /** Called when token refresh fails (e.g., clear stores, redirect to login). */
    19→  onRefreshFailure?: () => void;
    20→  /** Extra headers sent with every request (e.g., throttle bypass for testing). */
    21→  defaultHeaders?: Record<string, string>;
    22→};
    23→
    24→/**
    25→ * Create a configured axios instance for CipherBox API calls.
    26→ * No Zustand, no import.meta.env -- all config injected by consumer.
    27→ */
    28→export function createAxiosInstance(config: ApiClientConfig): AxiosInstance {
    29→  const instance = axios.create({
    30→    baseURL: config.baseUrl,
    31→    withCredentials: config.withCredentials ?? false,
    32→    headers: config.defaultHeaders,
    33→  });
    34→  instance.interceptors.request.use(async (reqConfig) => {
    35→    const token = await config.getAccessToken();
    36→    if (token) {
    37→      reqConfig.headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    38→    }
    39→    return reqConfig;
    40→  });
    41→  return instance;
    42→}
    43→
    44→/**
    45→ * Custom instance function for orval-generated code.
    46→ * Matches the signature orval expects from a mutator.
    47→ * The consumer must call setApiClientConfig() before using generated functions.
    48→ */
    49→let _config: ApiClientConfig | null = null;
    50→let _instance: AxiosInstance | null = null;
    51→
    52→export function setApiClientConfig(config: ApiClientConfig): void {
    53→  _config = config;
    54→  _instance = null; // Reset cached instance when config changes
    55→}
    56→
    57→export function getApiClientConfig(): ApiClientConfig {
    58→  if (!_config) throw new Error('API client not configured. Call setApiClientConfig() first.');
    59→  return _config;
    60→}
    61→
    62→// Shared refresh promise eliminates race condition where multiple concurrent
    63→// 401 responses each trigger their own refresh before the first completes.
    64→let _refreshPromise: Promise<string> | null = null;
    65→
    66→function getCachedInstance(): AxiosInstance {
    67→  if (!_instance) {
    68→    const clientConfig = getApiClientConfig();
    69→    _instance = axios.create({
    70→      baseURL: clientConfig.baseUrl,
    71→      withCredentials: clientConfig.withCredentials ?? false,
    72→      headers: clientConfig.defaultHeaders,
    73→    });
    74→
    75→    // 401 response interceptor: refresh token and retry once
    76→    if (clientConfig.refreshAccessToken) {
    77→      const refreshFn = clientConfig.refreshAccessToken;
    78→      const onFailure = clientConfig.onRefreshFailure;
    79→
    80→      _instance.interceptors.response.use(
    81→        (response) => response,
    82→        async (error: AxiosError) => {
    83→          const originalRequest = error.config as
    84→            | (AxiosRequestConfig & { _retry?: boolean })
    85→            | undefined;
    86→          if (!originalRequest) throw error;
    87→
    88→          // Don't retry refresh endpoint to avoid infinite loop
    89→          const isRefreshRequest = originalRequest.url?.includes('/auth/refresh');
    90→
    91→          if (error.response?.status === 401 && !originalRequest._retry && !isRefreshRequest) {
    92→            originalRequest._retry = true;
    93→
    94→            if (!_refreshPromise) {
    95→              // First 401 handler: create the refresh promise synchronously
    96→              _refreshPromise = refreshFn()
    97→                .catch((refreshError) => {
    98→                  onFailure?.();
    99→                  throw refreshError;
   100→                })
   101→                .finally(() => {
   102→                  _refreshPromise = null;
   103→                });
   104→            }
   105→
   106→            // All concurrent 401 handlers await the same promise.
   107→            const token = await _refreshPromise;
   108→            originalRequest.headers = originalRequest.headers ?? {};
   109→            originalRequest.headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
   110→            return getCachedInstance().request(originalRequest);
   111→          }
   112→
   113→          throw error;
   114→        }
   115→      );
   116→    }
   117→  }
   118→  return _instance;
   119→}
   120→
   121→export const customInstance = async <T>(
   122→  config: AxiosRequestConfig,
   123→  options?: AxiosRequestConfig
   124→): Promise<T> => {
   125→  const clientConfig = getApiClientConfig();
   126→  const token = await clientConfig.getAccessToken();
   127→  const instance = getCachedInstance();
   128→
   129→  const response = await instance.request<T>({
   130→    ...config,
   131→    ...options,
   132→    headers: {
   133→      ...config.headers,
   134→      ...options?.headers,
   135→      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
   136→    },
   137→  });
   138→
   139→  return response.data;
   140→};
   141→
   142→export default customInstance;
   143→
   144→export type ErrorType<Error> = AxiosError<Error>;
   145→export type BodyType<BodyData> = BodyData;
   146→
   147→// Re-export axios types that consumers need for IPFS upload (progress, cancel)
   148→export type { AxiosInstance, AxiosProgressEvent, CancelToken };
   149→export { default as axios } from 'axios';
   150→

     3→export default defineConfig({
     4→  cipherbox: {

     1→/**
     2→ * @cipherbox/sdk - Type definitions
     3→ *
     4→ * Client configuration and internal state types for the SDK.
     5→ * These types define the contract between the SDK and its consumers.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import type { TeeKeys } from '@cipherbox/sdk-core';
     9→import type { FolderChild, FolderMetadata } from '@cipherbox/core';
    10→import type { SentShareInfo } from './share';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Callbacks for share-aware key re-wrapping.
    14→ *
    15→ * The SDK uses these to discover active shares and store re-wrapped keys
    16→ * without taking a direct dependency on the shares API or stores.
    17→ */
    18→export type ShareCallbacks = {
    19→  /** Find active shares covering a folder (including ancestor shares). */
    20→  getCoveringShares: (folderIpnsName: string) => Promise<SentShareInfo[]>;
    21→  /** Store re-wrapped keys for a share via the API. */
    22→  addShareKeys: (
    23→    shareId: string,
    24→    keys: Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; encryptedKey: string }>
    25→  ) => Promise<void>;
    26→};
    27→
    28→/**
    29→ * Configuration for initializing a CipherBoxClient instance.
    30→ *
    31→ * Consumers provide authentication, keypair, and root folder details.
    32→ * The SDK manages everything else internally.
    33→ */
    34→export type CipherBoxClientConfig = {
    35→  /** Base URL for the CipherBox API (e.g., "https://api.cipherbox.cc") */
    36→  apiUrl: string;
    37→  /** Returns a valid access token. Consumer owns refresh logic. */
    38→  getAccessToken: () => Promise<string>;
    39→  /** User's vault keypair (secp256k1). Used for ECIES operations. */
    40→  vaultKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array };
    41→  /** Root folder IPNS name (k51.../bafzaa...) */
    42→  rootIpnsName: string;
    43→  /** Root folder key (AES-256, decrypted on login) */
    44→  rootFolderKey: Uint8Array;
    45→  /** TEE keys for IPNS key wrapping */
    46→  teeKeys?: TeeKeys;
    47→  /**
    48→   * Callbacks for share-aware operations (re-wrapping).
    49→   *
    50→   * When provided, the SDK will automatically re-wrap file and folder keys
    51→   * for share recipients after uploadFile() and createFolder(). This ensures
    52→   * recipients can decrypt items added to shared folders after the share
    53→   * was created.
    54→   *
    55→   * If not provided, re-wrapping is skipped (consumer must handle it).
    56→   */
    57→  shareCallbacks?: ShareCallbacks;
    58→  /** Callback when an operation starts */
    59→  onOperationStart?: (operation: string) => void;
    60→  /** Callback when an operation completes */
    61→  onOperationEnd?: (operation: string) => void;
    62→  /** Callback when an error occurs */
    63→  onError?: (error: Error) => void;
    64→};
    65→
    66→/**
    67→ * Internal state for a loaded folder.
    68→ *
    69→ * Tracks everything needed to read and update a folder's metadata.
    70→ * This is SDK-internal state -- consumers receive simplified events.
    71→ */
    72→export type FolderState = {
    73→  /** IPNS name identifying this folder */
    74→  ipnsName: string;
    75→  /** Decrypted AES-256 folder key */
    76→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
    77→  /** Ed25519 IPNS keypair for signing updates */
    78→  ipnsKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array };
    79→  /** Current IPNS sequence number (monotonically increasing) */
    80→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
    81→  /** Current folder children (files and subfolders) */
    82→  children: FolderChild[];
    83→  /** Full decrypted folder metadata, or null if not yet loaded */
    84→  metadata: FolderMetadata | null;
    85→  /** Timestamp (ms) of last successful load from IPNS */
    86→  lastLoadedAt: number;
    87→};
    88→

     1→/**
     2→ * @cipherbox/sdk - CipherBoxClient
     3→ *
     4→ * Stateful client orchestrating folder CRUD, file upload/download,
     5→ * and event-driven change notification. Wraps @cipherbox/sdk-core
     6→ * stateless functions with internal state management (FolderTree, KeyCache).
     7→ *
     8→ * Design principles:
     9→ * - Zero React/Zustand/browser dependencies
    10→ * - All state flows through typed events (SdkEvent)
    11→ * - Operations are wrapped with withOperation() for consistent
    12→ *   start/end/error event emission
    13→ * - Key material is cleared on destroy()
    14→ */
    15→
    16→import type { SdkContext, ProgressCallback, DownloadProgressCallback } from '@cipherbox/sdk-core';
    17→import * as sdkCore from '@cipherbox/sdk-core';
    18→import { clearBytes } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    19→import type { FolderChild } from '@cipherbox/core';
    20→import type { CipherBoxClientConfig, FolderState } from './types';
    21→import { SdkEventEmitter, type SdkEvent, type SdkEventHandler } from './events';
    22→import { FolderTree } from './state/folder-tree';
    23→import { KeyCache } from './state/key-cache';
    24→import * as binOps from './bin';
    25→import type { BinState } from './bin';
    26→import * as shareOps from './share';
    27→import type { SentShareInfo } from './share';
    28→
    29→/** Thrown when a bin operation is attempted before loadBin() has been called. */
    30→export class BinNotLoadedError extends Error {
    31→  constructor() {
    32→    super('Bin not loaded');
    33→    this.name = 'BinNotLoadedError';
    34→  }
    35→}
    36→
    37→export class CipherBoxClient {
    38→  private config: CipherBoxClientConfig;
    39→  private ctx: SdkContext;
    40→  private emitter: SdkEventEmitter;
    41→  private folderTree: FolderTree;
    42→  private keyCache: KeyCache;
    43→  private binState: BinState | null = null;
    44→  /** Internal copies of key material — zeroed on destroy() without affecting caller buffers */
    45→  private internalVaultKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array };
    46→  private internalRootFolderKey: Uint8Array;
    47→
    48→  constructor(config: CipherBoxClientConfig) {
    49→    // Defensive copy of key material so destroy() only zeroes our copies
    50→    this.internalVaultKeypair = {
    51→      publicKey: new Uint8Array(config.vaultKeypair.publicKey),
    52→      privateKey: new Uint8Array(config.vaultKeypair.privateKey),
    53→    };
    54→    this.internalRootFolderKey = new Uint8Array(config.rootFolderKey);
    55→    this.config = {
    56→      ...config,
    57→      vaultKeypair: this.internalVaultKeypair,
    58→      rootFolderKey: this.internalRootFolderKey,
    59→    };
    60→    this.ctx = {
    61→      apiUrl: config.apiUrl,
    62→      getAccessToken: config.getAccessToken,
    63→    };
    64→    this.emitter = new SdkEventEmitter();
    65→    this.folderTree = new FolderTree();
    66→    this.keyCache = new KeyCache();
    67→  }
    68→
    69→  // ---- Event subscription ----
    70→
    71→  /** Subscribe to SDK events. Returns an unsubscribe function. */
    72→  on(handler: SdkEventHandler): () => void {
    73→    return this.emitter.on(handler);
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  /** Unsubscribe a previously registered handler. */
    77→  off(handler: SdkEventHandler): void {
    78→    this.emitter.off(handler);
    79→  }
    80→
    81→  // ---- Lifecycle ----
    82→
    83→  // ---- Share re-wrapping (internal) ----
    84→
    85→  /**
    86→   * Re-wrap keys for share recipients after adding items to a folder.
    87→   *
    88→   * Queries the share callbacks for active shares covering the folder,
    89→   * then wraps each new item's plaintext key for each recipient.
    90→   * Failures are logged but do not propagate (re-wrapping is best-effort).
    91→   */
    92→  private async reWrapNewItems(
    93→    folderIpnsName: string,
    94→    items: Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; plaintextKey: Uint8Array }>
    95→  ): Promise<void> {
    96→    const callbacks = this.config.shareCallbacks;
    97→    if (!callbacks) return;
    98→
    99→    const coveringShares = await callbacks.getCoveringShares(folderIpnsName);
   100→    if (coveringShares.length === 0) return;
   101→
   102→    const { failedRecipients } = await shareOps.reWrapForRecipients({
   103→      coveringShares,
   104→      newItems: items,
   105→      addShareKeysFn: callbacks.addShareKeys,
   106→    });
   107→
   108→    if (failedRecipients.length > 0) {
   109→      console.warn(
   110→        `[SDK] Re-wrapping failed for ${failedRecipients.length} recipient(s):`,
   111→        failedRecipients.map((k) => k.slice(0, 10) + '...')
   112→      );
   113→      this.emitter.emit({
   114→        type: 'share:reWrapFailed',
   115→        folderIpnsName,
   116→        failedRecipients,
   117→      } as SdkEvent);
   118→    }
   119→  }
   120→
   121→  /**
   122→   * Destroy the client, clearing all sensitive state.
   123→   * After calling destroy(), the client instance should not be reused.
   124→   */
   125→  destroy(): void {
   126→    this.folderTree.clear();
   127→    this.keyCache.clear();
   128→    this.emitter.removeAll();
   129→    // Zero internal key copies (defense-in-depth; JS GC may retain copies)
   130→    // Only zeroes our copies, not the caller-provided buffers
   131→    this.internalVaultKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
   132→    this.internalVaultKeypair.publicKey.fill(0);
   133→    this.internalRootFolderKey.fill(0);
   134→    this.binState = null;
   135→  }
   136→
   137→  /**
   138→   * Check if the SDK already has state for a folder.
   139→   *
   140→   * @param ipnsName - Folder's IPNS name
   141→   * @returns true if the folder is registered in the SDK's FolderTree
   142→   */
   143→  hasFolder(ipnsName: string): boolean {
   144→    return this.folderTree.has(ipnsName);
   145→  }
   146→
   147→  /**
   148→   * Get the current sequence number for a folder in the SDK's internal state.
   149→   * Returns undefined if the folder is not registered.
   150→   */
   151→  getFolderSequenceNumber(ipnsName: string): bigint | undefined {
   152→    return this.folderTree.get(ipnsName)?.sequenceNumber;
   153→  }
   154→
   155→  /**
   156→   * Get the IPNS private key for a folder in the SDK's internal state.
   157→   * Returns undefined if the folder is not registered or has no key.
   158→   *
   159→   * Used by ensureFolderRegistered to preserve SDK's correct IPNS key
   160→   * when the store has an empty placeholder (SDK-created folders store
   161→   * keys internally, not in Zustand).
   162→   */
   163→  getFolderIpnsPrivateKey(ipnsName: string): Uint8Array | undefined {
   164→    const key = this.folderTree.get(ipnsName)?.ipnsKeypair?.privateKey;
   165→    // Return a copy so callers can't mutate internal state
   166→    return key && key.length > 0 ? new Uint8Array(key) : undefined;
   167→  }
   168→
   169→  /**
   170→   * Register an externally-loaded folder into the SDK's internal state.
   171→   *
   172→   * Used when folder metadata is loaded outside the SDK (e.g., by a navigation
   173→   * hook) but the SDK needs to know about the folder for mutation operations
   174→   * (create, rename, move, delete, upload).
   175→   *
   176→   * This is a bridge method for gradual SDK adoption -- eventually all folder
   177→   * loading should go through client.loadFolder().
   178→   *
   179→   * @param ipnsName - Folder's IPNS name
   180→   * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
   181→   * @param ipnsKeypair - Ed25519 keypair for IPNS signing
   182→   * @param children - Current folder children
   183→   * @param sequenceNumber - Current IPNS sequence number
   184→   */
   185→  registerFolder(
   186→    ipnsName: string,
   187→    folderKey: Uint8Array,
   188→    ipnsKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array },
   189→    children: FolderChild[],
   190→    sequenceNumber: bigint
   191→  ): void {
   192→    // Defensive copy so destroy() -> folderTree.clear() doesn't zero caller buffers
   193→    this.folderTree.set(ipnsName, {
   194→      ipnsName,
   195→      folderKey: new Uint8Array(folderKey),
   196→      ipnsKeypair: {
   197→        publicKey: new Uint8Array(ipnsKeypair.publicKey),
   198→        privateKey: new Uint8Array(ipnsKeypair.privateKey),
   199→      },
   200→      sequenceNumber,
   201→      children,
   202→      metadata: null,
   203→      lastLoadedAt: Date.now(),
   204→    });
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  // ---- Internal state access (for bin/share modules) ----
   208→
   209→  /** @internal Get the folder tree for bin/share operations */
   210→  getFolderTree(): FolderTree {
   211→    return this.folderTree;
   212→  }
   213→
   214→  /** @internal Get the SDK context */
   215→  getContext(): SdkContext {
   216→    return this.ctx;
   217→  }
   218→
   219→  /** @internal Get the client config */
   220→  getConfig(): CipherBoxClientConfig {
   221→    return this.config;
   222→  }
   223→
   224→  /** @internal Emit an event (used by bin/share modules) */
   225→  emitEvent(event: SdkEvent): void {
   226→    this.emitter.emit(event);
   227→  }
   228→
   229→  // ---- Folder operations ----
   230→
   231→  /**
   232→   * Load a folder's metadata from IPNS.
   233→   *
   234→   * Resolves the folder's IPNS record, decrypts metadata, stores state
   235→   * internally, and emits a 'folder:loaded' event.
   236→   *
   237→   * @param ipnsName - Folder's IPNS name
   238→   * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
   239→   * @param ipnsKeypair - Ed25519 keypair for IPNS signing
   240→   * @returns The loaded folder state, or null if IPNS record not found
   241→   */
   242→  async loadFolder(
   243→    ipnsName: string,
   244→    folderKey: Uint8Array,
   245→    ipnsKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array }
   246→  ): Promise<FolderState | null> {
   247→    return this.withOperation('loadFolder', async () => {
   248→      const result = await sdkCore.loadFolderMetadata({
   249→        ipnsName,
   250→        folderKey,
   251→        ctx: this.ctx,
   252→      });
   253→
   254→      if (!result) return null;
   255→
   256→      const state: FolderState = {
   257→        ipnsName,
   258→        folderKey,
   259→        ipnsKeypair,
   260→        sequenceNumber: result.sequenceNumber,
   261→        children: result.metadata.children,
   262→        metadata: result.metadata,
   263→        lastLoadedAt: Date.now(),
   264→      };
   265→
   266→      this.folderTree.set(ipnsName, state);
   267→
   268→      this.emitter.emit({
   269→        type: 'folder:loaded',
   270→        folderId: ipnsName,
   271→        ipnsName,
   272→        children: result.metadata.children,
   273→        sequenceNumber: result.sequenceNumber,
   274→      });
   275→
   276→      return state;
   277→    });
   278→  }
   279→
   280→  /**
   281→   * Create a new subfolder inside an existing folder.
   282→   *
   283→   * Generates IPNS keypair and folder key, wraps with user's public key,
   284→   * adds folder entry to parent metadata, publishes parent IPNS record,
   285→   * and publishes empty folder metadata for the new subfolder.
   286→   *
   287→   * @param parentIpnsName - IPNS name of the parent folder
   288→   * @param name - Name for the new subfolder
   289→   * @returns Created folder's UUID, IPNS name, folder key, and IPNS private key
   290→   */
   291→  async createFolder(
   292→    parentIpnsName: string,
   293→    name: string
   294→  ): Promise<{ id: string; ipnsName: string; folderKey: Uint8Array; ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array }> {
   295→    return this.withOperation('createFolder', async () => {
   296→      const parent = this.folderTree.get(parentIpnsName);
   297→      if (!parent) throw new Error('Parent folder not loaded');
   298→
   299→      // 1. Check for duplicate name before allocating keys
   300→      if (parent.children.some((child) => child.name === name)) {
   301→        throw new Error('An item with this name already exists');
   302→      }
   303→
   304→      // 2. Create subfolder (generates keys, wraps with user public key)
   305→      const { folder, ipnsPrivateKey, folderKey, encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, keyEpoch } =
   306→        await sdkCore.createSubfolder({
   307→          name,
   308→          userPublicKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   309→          teeKeys: this.config.teeKeys,
   310→        });
   311→
   312→      try {
   313→        // 3. Add folder entry to parent's children
   314→        const updatedChildren: FolderChild[] = [...parent.children, folder];
   315→
   316→        // 4. Update parent metadata and publish
   317→        const { newSequenceNumber } = await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
   318→          children: updatedChildren,
   319→          folderKey: parent.folderKey,
   320→          ipnsPrivateKey: parent.ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   321→          ipnsName: parentIpnsName,
   322→          sequenceNumber: parent.sequenceNumber,
   323→          ctx: this.ctx,
   324→          encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   325→          keyEpoch,
   326→        });
   327→
   328→        // 5. Update parent state
   329→        parent.children = updatedChildren;
   330→        parent.sequenceNumber = newSequenceNumber;
   331→        parent.lastLoadedAt = Date.now();
   332→        this.folderTree.set(parentIpnsName, parent);
   333→
   334→        // 6. Publish empty metadata for the new subfolder
   335→        await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
   336→          children: [],
   337→          folderKey,
   338→          ipnsPrivateKey,
   339→          ipnsName: folder.ipnsName,
   340→          sequenceNumber: 0n,
   341→          ctx: this.ctx,
   342→          encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   343→          keyEpoch,
   344→        });
   345→
   346→        // 7. Emit parent folder updated event
   347→        this.emitter.emit({
   348→          type: 'folder:updated',
   349→          folderId: parentIpnsName,
   350→          ipnsName: parentIpnsName,
   351→          children: updatedChildren,
   352→          sequenceNumber: newSequenceNumber,
   353→        });
   354→
   355→        // 8. Re-wrap subfolder key for share recipients (non-blocking)
   356→        if (this.config.shareCallbacks) {
   357→          // Copy folderKey for the detached task — caller may wipe the returned buffer
   358→          const folderKeyCopy = new Uint8Array(folderKey);
   359→          this.reWrapNewItems(parentIpnsName, [
   360→            { keyType: 'folder', itemId: folder.id, plaintextKey: folderKeyCopy },
   361→          ])
   362→            .catch((err) => {
   363→              console.warn('[SDK] Post-createFolder re-wrapping failed:', err);
   364→            })
   365→            .finally(() => {
   366→              clearBytes(folderKeyCopy);
   367→            });
   368→        }
   369→
   370→        return { id: folder.id, ipnsName: folder.ipnsName, folderKey, ipnsPrivateKey };
   371→      } catch (err) {
   372→        // Clear plaintext keys on failure — they won't reach the caller
   373→        clearBytes(folderKey);
   374→        clearBytes(ipnsPrivateKey);
   375→        throw err;
   376→      }
   377→    });
   378→  }
   379→
   380→  /**
   381→   * Rename a child entry (folder or file) in a folder.
   382→   *
   383→   * Updates the name in the folder's metadata and publishes the change.
   384→   *
   385→   * @param folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder containing the item
   386→   * @param childId - ID of the child to rename
   387→   * @param newName - New name for the child
   388→   */
   389→  async renameItem(folderIpnsName: string, childId: string, newName: string): Promise<void> {
   390→    return this.withOperation('renameItem', async () => {
   391→      const folder = this.folderTree.get(folderIpnsName);
   392→      if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');
   393→
   394→      // 1. Rename in metadata (pure operation)
   395→      const { updatedChildren } = sdkCore.renameInFolder({
   396→        children: folder.children,
   397→        childId,
   398→        newName,
   399→      });
   400→
   401→      // 2. Publish updated metadata
   402→      const { newSequenceNumber } = await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
   403→        children: updatedChildren,
   404→        folderKey: folder.folderKey,
   405→        ipnsPrivateKey: folder.ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   406→        ipnsName: folderIpnsName,
   407→        sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
   408→        ctx: this.ctx,
   409→      });
   410→
   411→      // 3. Update internal state
   412→      folder.children = updatedChildren;
   413→      folder.sequenceNumber = newSequenceNumber;
   414→      folder.lastLoadedAt = Date.now();
   415→      this.folderTree.set(folderIpnsName, folder);
   416→
   417→      // 4. Emit update event
   418→      this.emitter.emit({
   419→        type: 'folder:updated',
   420→        folderId: folderIpnsName,
   421→        ipnsName: folderIpnsName,
   422→        children: updatedChildren,
   423→        sequenceNumber: newSequenceNumber,
   424→      });
   425→    });
   426→  }
   427→
   428→  /**
   429→   * Move a child entry between two folders.
   430→   *
   431→   * Removes from source, adds to destination, publishes both IPNS records
   432→   * (destination first for crash safety -- add-before-remove pattern).
   433→   *
   434→   * @param sourceIpnsName - IPNS name of the source folder
   435→   * @param destIpnsName - IPNS name of the destination folder
   436→   * @param childId - ID of the child to move
   437→   */
   438→  async moveItem(sourceIpnsName: string, destIpnsName: string, childId: string): Promise<void> {
   439→    return this.withOperation('moveItem', async () => {
   440→      const source = this.folderTree.get(sourceIpnsName);
   441→      const dest = this.folderTree.get(destIpnsName);
   442→      if (!source) throw new Error('Source folder not loaded');
   443→      if (!dest) throw new Error('Destination folder not loaded');
   444→
   445→      // 1. Compute updated children for both folders (pure operation)
   446→      const { updatedSourceChildren, updatedDestChildren } = sdkCore.moveItem({
   447→        sourceChildren: source.children,
   448→        destChildren: dest.children,
   449→        childId,
   450→      });
   451→
   452→      // 2. Publish destination first (add-before-remove for crash safety)
   453→      const destResult = await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
   454→        children: updatedDestChildren,
   455→        folderKey: dest.folderKey,
   456→        ipnsPrivateKey: dest.ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   457→        ipnsName: destIpnsName,
   458→        sequenceNumber: dest.sequenceNumber,
   459→        ctx: this.ctx,
   460→      });
   461→
   462→      // 3. Publish source (remove)
   463→      const sourceResult = await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
   464→        children: updatedSourceChildren,
   465→        folderKey: source.folderKey,
   466→        ipnsPrivateKey: source.ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   467→        ipnsName: sourceIpnsName,
   468→        sequenceNumber: source.sequenceNumber,
   469→        ctx: this.ctx,
   470→      });
   471→
   472→      // 4. Update internal state
   473→      source.children = updatedSourceChildren;
   474→      source.sequenceNumber = sourceResult.newSequenceNumber;
   475→      dest.children = updatedDestChildren;
   476→      dest.sequenceNumber = destResult.newSequenceNumber;
   477→      this.folderTree.set(sourceIpnsName, source);
   478→      this.folderTree.set(destIpnsName, dest);
   479→
   480→      // 5. Emit events for both folders
   481→      this.emitter.emit({
   482→        type: 'folder:updated',
   483→        folderId: sourceIpnsName,
   484→        ipnsName: sourceIpnsName,
   485→        children: updatedSourceChildren,
   486→        sequenceNumber: sourceResult.newSequenceNumber,
   487→      });
   488→      this.emitter.emit({
   489→        type: 'folder:updated',
   490→        folderId: destIpnsName,
   491→        ipnsName: destIpnsName,
   492→        children: updatedDestChildren,
   493→        sequenceNumber: destResult.newSequenceNumber,
   494→      });
   495→    });
   496→  }
   497→
   498→  /**
   499→   * Delete a child entry from a folder's metadata.
   500→   *
   501→   * This is a simple metadata delete -- it does NOT move to bin.
   502→   * Use deleteToBin() for soft-delete with bin support.
   503→   *
   504→   * @param folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder containing the item
   505→   * @param childId - ID of the child to delete
   506→   * @returns The removed child entry
   507→   */
   508→  async deleteItem(folderIpnsName: string, childId: string): Promise<{ removedItem: FolderChild }> {
   509→    return this.withOperation('deleteItem', async () => {
   510→      const folder = this.folderTree.get(folderIpnsName);
   511→      if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');
   512→
   513→      // 1. Remove from metadata (pure operation)
   514→      const { updatedChildren, removedItem } = sdkCore.deleteFromFolder({
   515→        children: folder.children,
   516→        childId,
   517→      });
   518→
   519→      // 2. Publish updated metadata
   520→      const { newSequenceNumber } = await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
   521→        children: updatedChildren,
   522→        folderKey: folder.folderKey,
   523→        ipnsPrivateKey: folder.ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   524→        ipnsName: folderIpnsName,
   525→        sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
   526→        ctx: this.ctx,
   527→      });
   528→
   529→      // 3. Update internal state
   530→      folder.children = updatedChildren;
   531→      folder.sequenceNumber = newSequenceNumber;
   532→      folder.lastLoadedAt = Date.now();
   533→      this.folderTree.set(folderIpnsName, folder);
   534→
   535→      // 4. Emit update event
   536→      this.emitter.emit({
   537→        type: 'folder:updated',
   538→        folderId: folderIpnsName,
   539→        ipnsName: folderIpnsName,
   540→        children: updatedChildren,
   541→        sequenceNumber: newSequenceNumber,
   542→      });
   543→
   544→      return { removedItem };
   545→    });
   546→  }
   547→
   548→  // ---- File operations ----
   549→
   550→  /**
   551→   * Upload a file to a folder.
   552→   *
   553→   * Encrypts the file, uploads to IPFS, creates per-file IPNS metadata,
   554→   * adds a FilePointer to the parent folder's metadata, and publishes.
   555→   *
   556→   * @param folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the target folder
   557→   * @param data - Raw file content as Uint8Array
   558→   * @param fileName - Display name for the file
   559→   * @param mimeType - MIME type of the file
   560→   * @param onProgress - Optional upload progress callback
   561→   * @returns CID of the uploaded encrypted file
   562→   */
   563→  async uploadFile(
   564→    folderIpnsName: string,
   565→    data: Uint8Array,
   566→    fileName: string,
   567→    mimeType: string,
   568→    onProgress?: ProgressCallback
   569→  ): Promise<{ cid: string }> {
   570→    return this.withOperation('uploadFile', async () => {
   571→      const folder = this.folderTree.get(folderIpnsName);
   572→      if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');
   573→
   574→      if (folder.children.some((child) => child.name === fileName)) {
   575→        throw new Error('An item with this name already exists');
   576→      }
   577→
   578→      const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();
   579→
   580→      // 1. Encrypt and upload file, create file metadata
   581→      const uploadResult = await sdkCore.uploadFile({
   582→        data,
   583→        fileId,
   584→        mimeType,
   585→        folderKey: folder.folderKey,
   586→        userPublicKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   587→        ctx: this.ctx,
   588→        onProgress,
   589→        teeKeys: this.config.teeKeys,
   590→      });
   591→
   592→      try {
   593→        // 2. Add FilePointer to folder's children
   594→        const { updatedChildren } = sdkCore.addFilePointerToFolder({
   595→          children: folder.children,
   596→          fileId,
   597→          fileName,
   598→          fileMetaIpnsName: uploadResult.fileMetaIpnsName,
   599→          ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: uploadResult.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   600→        });
   601→
   602→        // 3. Batch publish: file metadata IPNS + folder metadata IPNS
   603→        await sdkCore.batchPublishIpnsRecords([uploadResult.ipnsRecord]);
   604→
   605→        const { newSequenceNumber } = await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
   606→          children: updatedChildren,
   607→          folderKey: folder.folderKey,
   608→          ipnsPrivateKey: folder.ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   609→          ipnsName: folderIpnsName,
   610→          sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
   611→          ctx: this.ctx,
   612→        });
   613→
   614→        // 4. Update internal state
   615→        folder.children = updatedChildren;
   616→        folder.sequenceNumber = newSequenceNumber;
   617→        folder.lastLoadedAt = Date.now();
   618→        this.folderTree.set(folderIpnsName, folder);
   619→
   620→        // 5. Emit events
   621→        this.emitter.emit({
   622→          type: 'file:uploaded',
   623→          folderId: folderIpnsName,
   624→          fileName,
   625→          cid: uploadResult.cid,
   626→        });
   627→        this.emitter.emit({
   628→          type: 'folder:updated',
   629→          folderId: folderIpnsName,
   630→          ipnsName: folderIpnsName,
   631→          children: updatedChildren,
   632→          sequenceNumber: newSequenceNumber,
   633→        });
   634→
   635→        // 6. Re-wrap file key for share recipients (best-effort)
   636→        try {
   637→          if (this.config.shareCallbacks) {
   638→            await this.reWrapNewItems(folderIpnsName, [
   639→              { keyType: 'file', itemId: fileId, plaintextKey: uploadResult.fileKey },
   640→            ]);
   641→          }
   642→        } catch (err) {
   643→          console.warn('[SDK] Post-upload re-wrapping failed:', err);
   644→        }
   645→
   646→        return { cid: uploadResult.cid };
   647→      } finally {
   648→        clearBytes(uploadResult.fileKey);
   649→      }
   650→    });
   651→  }
   652→
   653→  /**
   654→   * Download a file using its per-file IPNS metadata.
   655→   *
   656→   * Resolves the file's IPNS record, decrypts the metadata with the
   657→   * folder key, then downloads and decrypts the file content.
   658→   * This is the primary download path for v2 folder metadata.
   659→   *
   660→   * @param fileMetaIpnsName - IPNS name of the file's metadata record
   661→   * @param folderKey - Parent folder's decrypted AES-256 key
   662→   * @param onProgress - Optional download progress callback
   663→   * @returns Decrypted file content
   664→   */
   665→  async downloadFromIpns(
   666→    fileMetaIpnsName: string,
   667→    folderKey: Uint8Array,
   668→    onProgress?: DownloadProgressCallback
   669→  ): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   670→    return this.withOperation('downloadFromIpns', async () => {
   671→      // 1. Resolve per-file IPNS to get FileMetadata
   672→      const resolved = await sdkCore.resolveFileMetadata(fileMetaIpnsName, folderKey, this.ctx);
   673→
   674→      // 2. Download and decrypt file content
   675→      const plaintext = await sdkCore.downloadAndDecrypt({
   676→        cid: resolved.metadata.cid,
   677→        fileKeyEncrypted: resolved.metadata.fileKeyEncrypted,
   678→        fileIv: resolved.metadata.fileIv,
   679→        userPrivateKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   680→        encryptionMode: resolved.metadata.encryptionMode,
   681→        ctx: this.ctx,
   682→        onProgress,
   683→      });
   684→
   685→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'file:downloaded', cid: resolved.metadata.cid });
   686→      return plaintext;
   687→    });
   688→  }
   689→
   690→  /**
   691→   * Download and decrypt a file from IPFS.
   692→   *
   693→   * Fetches the encrypted file content, unwraps the file key using the
   694→   * user's private key, and decrypts the content.
   695→   *
   696→   * @param cid - IPFS CID of the encrypted file content
   697→   * @param fileKeyEncrypted - Hex-encoded ECIES-wrapped file key
   698→   * @param fileIv - Hex-encoded IV used for encryption
   699→   * @param encryptionMode - 'GCM' (default) or 'CTR'
   700→   * @param onProgress - Optional download progress callback
   701→   * @returns Decrypted file content
   702→   */
   703→  async downloadFile(
   704→    cid: string,
   705→    fileKeyEncrypted: string,
   706→    fileIv: string,
   707→    encryptionMode?: 'GCM' | 'CTR',
   708→    onProgress?: DownloadProgressCallback
   709→  ): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   710→    return this.withOperation('downloadFile', async () => {
   711→      const plaintext = await sdkCore.downloadAndDecrypt({
   712→        cid,
   713→        fileKeyEncrypted,
   714→        fileIv,
   715→        userPrivateKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   716→        encryptionMode,
   717→        ctx: this.ctx,
   718→        onProgress,
   719→      });
   720→
   721→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'file:downloaded', cid });
   722→
   723→      return plaintext;
   724→    });
   725→  }
   726→
   727→  // ---- Bin operations ----
   728→
   729→  /**
   730→   * Load the recycle bin metadata.
   731→   *
   732→   * Always returns a BinState — if no bin IPNS record exists yet,
   733→   * returns empty state so deleteToBin can create the first record.
   734→   */
   735→  async loadBin(): Promise<BinState> {
   736→    return this.withOperation('loadBin', async () => {
   737→      const result = await binOps.loadBin({
   738→        binCtx: this.getBinContext(),
   739→      });
   740→
   741→      this.binState = result;
   742→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'bin:updated', entries: result.entries });
   743→
   744→      return result;
   745→    });
   746→  }
   747→
   748→  /**
   749→   * Soft-delete an item by moving it to the recycle bin.
   750→   *
   751→   * Removes the item from the folder's metadata, adds a BinEntry,
   752→   * and publishes both folder and bin IPNS records.
   753→   *
   754→   * @param folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder containing the item
   755→   * @param childId - ID of the child to delete
   756→   * @param parentPath - Breadcrumb path for restore (e.g., "My Vault / Documents")
   757→   */
   758→  async deleteToBin(folderIpnsName: string, childId: string, parentPath: string): Promise<void> {
   759→    return this.withOperation('deleteToBin', async () => {
   760→      if (!this.binState) throw new BinNotLoadedError();
   761→
   762→      const { updatedBinState } = await binOps.addToBin({
   763→        folderIpnsName,
   764→        childId,
   765→        parentPath,
   766→        folderTree: this.folderTree,
   767→        binState: this.binState,
   768→        binCtx: this.getBinContext(),
   769→      });
   770→
   771→      this.binState = updatedBinState;
   772→
   773→      // Emit events
   774→      const folderState = this.folderTree.get(folderIpnsName);
   775→      this.emitter.emit({
   776→        type: 'folder:updated',
   777→        folderId: folderIpnsName,
   778→        ipnsName: folderIpnsName,
   779→        children: folderState?.children ?? [],
   780→        sequenceNumber: folderState?.sequenceNumber ?? 0n,
   781→      });
   782→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'bin:updated', entries: updatedBinState.entries });
   783→    });
   784→  }
   785→
   786→  /**
   787→   * Restore an item from the recycle bin to its target folder.
   788→   *
   789→   * @param entryId - ID of the bin entry to restore
   790→   * @param targetFolderIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder to restore to
   791→   */
   792→  async restoreFromBin(entryId: string, targetFolderIpnsName: string): Promise<void> {
   793→    return this.withOperation('restoreFromBin', async () => {
   794→      if (!this.binState) throw new BinNotLoadedError();
   795→
   796→      const { updatedBinState } = await binOps.restoreFromBin({
   797→        entryId,
   798→        targetFolderIpnsName,
   799→        folderTree: this.folderTree,
   800→        binState: this.binState,
   801→        binCtx: this.getBinContext(),
   802→      });
   803→
   804→      this.binState = updatedBinState;
   805→
   806→      // Emit events
   807→      const targetState = this.folderTree.get(targetFolderIpnsName);
   808→      this.emitter.emit({
   809→        type: 'folder:updated',
   810→        folderId: targetFolderIpnsName,
   811→        ipnsName: targetFolderIpnsName,
   812→        children: targetState?.children ?? [],
   813→        sequenceNumber: targetState?.sequenceNumber ?? 0n,
   814→      });
   815→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'bin:updated', entries: updatedBinState.entries });
   816→    });
   817→  }
   818→
   819→  /**
   820→   * Permanently delete a bin entry (unpin CIDs, remove from bin).
   821→   *
   822→   * @param entryId - ID of the bin entry to permanently delete
   823→   */
   824→  async permanentDelete(entryId: string): Promise<void> {
   825→    return this.withOperation('permanentDelete', async () => {
   826→      if (!this.binState) throw new BinNotLoadedError();
   827→
   828→      const { updatedBinState } = await binOps.permanentDeleteFromBin({
   829→        entryId,
   830→        binState: this.binState,
   831→        binCtx: this.getBinContext(),
   832→      });
   833→
   834→      this.binState = updatedBinState;
   835→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'bin:updated', entries: updatedBinState.entries });
   836→    });
   837→  }
   838→
   839→  /**
   840→   * Permanently delete all bin entries.
   841→   */
   842→  async emptyBin(): Promise<void> {
   843→    return this.withOperation('emptyBin', async () => {
   844→      if (!this.binState) throw new BinNotLoadedError();
   845→
   846→      const { updatedBinState } = await binOps.emptyBin({
   847→        binState: this.binState,
   848→        binCtx: this.getBinContext(),
   849→      });
   850→
   851→      this.binState = updatedBinState;
   852→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'bin:updated', entries: [] });
   853→    });
   854→  }
   855→
   856→  // ---- Share operations ----
   857→
   858→  /**
   859→   * Create an ECIES-wrapped key for sharing a folder with a recipient.
   860→   *
   861→   * @param folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder to share
   862→   * @param recipientPublicKey - Recipient's secp256k1 public key
   863→   * @returns Hex-encoded wrapped key for the recipient
   864→   */
   865→  async shareFolder(
   866→    folderIpnsName: string,
   867→    recipientPublicKey: Uint8Array
   868→  ): Promise<{ encryptedKey: string }> {
   869→    return this.withOperation('shareFolder', async () => {
   870→      const folder = this.folderTree.get(folderIpnsName);
   871→      if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');
   872→
   873→      return shareOps.createShareKey({
   874→        folderKey: folder.folderKey,
   875→        recipientPublicKey,
   876→        folderIpnsName,
   877→        shareCtx: this.getShareContext(),
   878→      });
   879→    });
   880→  }
   881→
   882→  /**
   883→   * Revoke a share (soft-delete).
   884→   *
   885→   * @param shareId - Share ID to revoke
   886→   * @param revokeShareFn - Function to call the API revoke endpoint
   887→   */
   888→  async revokeShare(
   889→    shareId: string,
   890→    revokeShareFn: (shareId: string) => Promise<void>
   891→  ): Promise<void> {
   892→    return this.withOperation('revokeShare', async () => {
   893→      await shareOps.revokeShare({ shareId, revokeShareFn });
   894→    });
   895→  }
   896→
   897→  /**
   898→   * Re-wrap keys for share recipients after adding items to a shared folder.
   899→   *
   900→   * @param coveringShares - Active shares covering the folder
   901→   * @param newItems - New items whose keys need re-wrapping
   902→   * @param addShareKeysFn - Function to add wrapped keys to a share via API
   903→   */
   904→  async reWrapForRecipients(
   905→    coveringShares: SentShareInfo[],
   906→    newItems: Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; plaintextKey: Uint8Array }>,
   907→    addShareKeysFn: (
   908→      shareId: string,
   909→      keys: Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; encryptedKey: string }>
   910→    ) => Promise<void>
   911→  ): Promise<{ failedRecipients: string[] }> {
   912→    return this.withOperation('reWrapForRecipients', async () => {
   913→      return shareOps.reWrapForRecipients({
   914→        coveringShares,
   915→        newItems,
   916→        addShareKeysFn,
   917→      });
   918→    });
   919→  }
   920→
   921→  // ---- Private helpers ----
   922→
   923→  /** Build bin operation context from client config */
   924→  private getBinContext(): binOps.BinOperationContext {
   925→    return {
   926→      ctx: this.ctx,
   927→      userPrivateKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   928→      userPublicKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   929→      rootFolderKey: this.config.rootFolderKey,
   930→      teeKeys: this.config.teeKeys,
   931→    };
   932→  }
   933→
   934→  /** Build share operation context from client config */
   935→  private getShareContext(): shareOps.ShareOperationContext {
   936→    return {
   937→      ctx: this.ctx,
   938→      userPrivateKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   939→      userPublicKey: this.config.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   940→    };
   941→  }
   942→
   943→  // ---- Operation wrapper ----
   944→
   945→  /**
   946→   * Wrap an async operation with event emission and error handling.
   947→   *
   948→   * Emits 'operation:start' before, 'operation:end' on success (with duration),
   949→   * and 'error' on failure. Also calls the config callbacks if provided.
   950→   */
   951→  private async withOperation<T>(name: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
   952→    const start = Date.now();
   953→    this.notifySafely(() => this.config.onOperationStart?.(name));
   954→    this.emitter.emit({ type: 'operation:start', operation: name });
   955→
   956→    try {
   957→      const result = await fn();
   958→      const durationMs = Date.now() - start;
   959→      this.notifySafely(() => this.config.onOperationEnd?.(name));
   960→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'operation:end', operation: name, durationMs });
   961→      return result;
   962→    } catch (error) {
   963→      this.notifySafely(() => this.config.onError?.(error as Error));
   964→      this.emitter.emit({ type: 'error', operation: name, error: error as Error });
   965→      throw error;
   966→    }
   967→  }
   968→
   969→  private notifySafely(fn: () => void): void {
   970→    try {
   971→      fn();
   972→    } catch (err) {
   973→      console.warn('[SDK] Lifecycle callback threw:', err);
   974→    }
   975→  }
   976→}
   977→

## Summary: `customInstance` Usage in CipherBox

Here's a thorough analysis of how `customInstance` from the orval-generated API client is imported and used throughout the codebase:

### Core Definition

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/api-client/src/instance.ts`

The `customInstance` function is a mutator for orval-generated code that wraps axios calls:

```typescript
export const customInstance = async <T>(
  config: AxiosRequestConfig,
  options?: AxiosRequestConfig
): Promise<T> => {
  const clientConfig = getApiClientConfig();
  const token = await clientConfig.getAccessToken();
  const instance = getCachedInstance();

  const response = await instance.request<T>({
    ...config,
    ...options,
    headers: {
      ...config.headers,
      ...options?.headers,
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    },
  });

  return response.data;
};

export default customInstance;
```

Key features:
- Handles token acquisition via `getApiClientConfig().getAccessToken()`
- Manages 401 refresh logic with deduplication (shared Promise)
- Returns typed response data directly (not the full axios response)

### Orval Configuration

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/api-client/orval.config.ts`

```typescript
override: {
  mutator: {
    path: './src/instance.ts',
    name: 'customInstance',
  },
}
```

This tells orval to use `customInstance` as the mutator function for all generated API calls.

### Public Export

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/api-client/src/index.ts`

```typescript
export {
  customInstance,
  createAxiosInstance,
  setApiClientConfig,
  getApiClientConfig,
} from './instance';
```

`customInstance` is publicly exported so tests and edge cases can use it directly.

import { setApiClientConfig, authControllerRefresh } from '@cipherbox/api-client';

setApiClientConfig({
  baseUrl: apiUrl,
  getAccessToken: async () => useAuthStore.getState().accessToken || '',
  withCredentials: true,
  refreshAccessToken: async () => {

});

Every generated API function follows this pattern:

The beauty of this design is that all 100+ generated API functions automatically get token handling, 401 refresh/retry, and error management without code duplication.

## Summary: SdkContext Usage in SDK Core

Based on my thorough analysis of `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/`, here's how `SdkContext` flows through the codebase:

### SdkContext Type Definition
**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/types.ts`

```typescript
export type SdkContext = {
  apiUrl: string;  // Base URL for the CipherBox API (e.g., "http://localhost:3000")
  getAccessToken: () => Promise<string>;  // Returns a valid access token
};
```

---

### Files Using SdkContext and Their Function Signatures

#### 1. **IPFS Operations** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/ipfs/index.ts`)

Three functions accept `ctx: SdkContext`:

**`addToIpfs(ctx, encryptedData, onProgress?, cancelToken?)`**
- Accesses: `ctx.getAccessToken()` → retrieves Bearer token
- Accesses: `ctx.apiUrl` → constructs `${ctx.apiUrl}/ipfs/upload` endpoint
- Purpose: Upload encrypted data to IPFS via backend relay with progress tracking

**`fetchFromIpfs(ctx, cid, onProgress?)`**
- Accesses: `ctx.getAccessToken()` → retrieves Bearer token
- Accesses: `ctx.apiUrl` → constructs `${ctx.apiUrl}/ipfs/{cid}` endpoint
- Purpose: Fetch encrypted data from IPFS with download progress tracking

**`unpinFromIpfs(ctx, cid)`**
- Accesses: `ctx.getAccessToken()` → retrieves Bearer token
- Accesses: `ctx.apiUrl` → constructs `${ctx.apiUrl}/ipfs/unpin` endpoint
- Purpose: Unpin (delete) a file from IPFS

---

#### 2. **Upload Operations** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/upload/index.ts`)

**`uploadFile(params)`** where `params.ctx: SdkContext`
- Passes `ctx` to `addToIpfs(params.ctx, ciphertext, params.onProgress)` 
- Passes `ctx` to `createFileMetadata(..., ctx: params.ctx, ...)`
- Accesses indirectly: `ctx.getAccessToken()` and `ctx.apiUrl` (via nested function calls)
- Purpose: Encrypt and upload a single file to IPFS with file metadata creation

---

#### 3. **Download Operations** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/download/index.ts`)

**`downloadAndDecrypt(params)`** where `params.ctx: SdkContext`
- Passes `ctx` to `fetchFromIpfs(params.ctx, params.cid, params.onProgress)`
- Accesses indirectly: `ctx.getAccessToken()` and `ctx.apiUrl` (via nested function call)
- Purpose: Fetch and decrypt a file from IPFS

---

#### 4. **Folder Operations** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts`)

**`fetchAndDecryptMetadata(cid, folderKey, ctx)`**
- Passes `ctx` to `fetchFromIpfs(ctx, cid)`
- Purpose: Fetch and decrypt folder metadata from IPFS

**`loadFolderMetadata(params)` where `params.ctx: SdkContext`**
- Passes `params.ctx` to `fetchAndDecryptMetadata(resolved.cid, params.folderKey, params.ctx)`
- Purpose: Load folder metadata from IPNS (resolves name, then fetches/decrypts)

**`updateFolderMetadataAndPublish(params)` where `params.ctx: SdkContext`**
- Passes `params.ctx` to `addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes)`
- Does NOT pass `ctx` to `createAndPublishIpnsRecord()` (IPNS publish calls API directly)
- Purpose: Upload encrypted folder metadata and publish IPNS record

**Note:** Functions like `renameInFolder()`, `deleteFromFolder()`, `addFilePointerToFolder()`, `moveItem()` are pure metadata operations and **do NOT use `ctx`** — they operate on in-memory data structures only.

---

#### 5. **File Metadata Operations** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/file/index.ts`)

**`createFileMetadata(params)` where `params.ctx: SdkContext`**
- Passes `params.ctx` to `addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes)`
- Does NOT pass `ctx` to `createIpnsRecord()` or marshaling (local crypto operations)
- Does NOT pass `ctx` to API publish call (handled by caller or TEE enrollment)
- Purpose: Create per-file IPNS metadata record

**`resolveFileMetadata(fileMetaIpnsName, folderKey, ctx)`**
- Passes `ctx` to `fetchFromIpfs(ctx, resolved.cid)`
- Purpose: Resolve file metadata from IPNS and fetch/decrypt

**`updateFileMetadata(params)` where `params.ctx: SdkContext`**
- Passes `params.ctx` to `addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes)`
- Purpose: Update file metadata and create new IPNS record

---

#### 6. **IPNS Operations** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/ipns/index.ts`)

**`createAndPublishIpnsRecord(params)` — NO SdkContext parameter**
- Uses `ipnsControllerPublishRecord()` from `@cipherbox/api-client` directly
- API client is pre-configured with auth/baseURL elsewhere in the app
- Purpose: Create and publish a single IPNS record

**`batchPublishIpnsRecords(records)` — NO SdkContext parameter**
- Uses `ipnsControllerPublishBatch()` from `@cipherbox/api-client` directly
- Purpose: Batch publish multiple IPNS records

**`resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName)` — NO SdkContext parameter**
- Uses `ipnsControllerResolveRecord()` from `@cipherbox/api-client` directly
- Purpose: Resolve IPNS name to CID (calls API for delegated routing)

**`verifyIpnsSignature(signatureV2, data, pubKey)` — NO SdkContext parameter**
- Pure crypto operation (no network access)
- Purpose: Verify Ed25519 signature on IPNS record

---

### SdkContext Flow Summary

```
SdkContext
├── apiUrl: string
│   └── Used to construct API endpoints for IPFS operations
│       ├── ${apiUrl}/ipfs/upload (addToIpfs)
│       ├── ${apiUrl}/ipfs/{cid} (fetchFromIpfs)
│       └── ${apiUrl}/ipfs/unpin (unpinFromIpfs)
│
└── getAccessToken: () => Promise<string>
    └── Called before every IPFS API call
        ├── Added as Bearer token in Authorization header
        └── Used in: addToIpfs, fetchFromIpfs, unpinFromIpfs

Call Chain Example:
uploadFile(ctx) 
  → addToIpfs(ctx) [uses ctx.apiUrl + ctx.getAccessToken()]
  → createFileMetadata(ctx) [passes to addToIpfs again]
    → addToIpfs(ctx) [uses ctx.apiUrl + ctx.getAccessToken()]
```

---

### Key Observations

1. **Limited context scope:** `SdkContext` contains only two properties used for IPFS relay access (URL + token).

2. **IPNS does NOT use SdkContext:** The IPNS functions (`createAndPublishIpnsRecord`, `resolveIpnsRecord`, `batchPublishIpnsRecords`) directly use the pre-configured `@cipherbox/api-client` instead of accepting `ctx`. This is intentional — the API client is a singleton configured at app initialization.

3. **Crypto operations don't need ctx:** Purely local operations (`createFileMetadata` metadata encryption, `renameInFolder`, etc.) don't accept `ctx`.

4. **Token is fetched fresh on each IPFS call:** `ctx.getAccessToken()` is a promise, called before each request rather than using a cached token.

5. **Context is passed transitively:** Functions that call IPFS operations (like `uploadFile`, `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish`) receive `ctx` and pass it down to IPFS functions, rather than obtaining it themselves.

---

### Test Verification
**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/__tests__/helpers.ts`

```typescript
function createMockContext(): SdkContext {
  return {
    apiUrl: 'http://localhost:3000',
    getAccessToken: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue('test-token'),
  };
}
```

This confirms the context structure and shows that tests mock both properties.

     1→import axios, { type AxiosProgressEvent, type CancelToken } from 'axios';
     2→import type {
     3→  SdkContext,
     4→  IpfsAddResult,
     5→  ProgressCallback,
     6→  DownloadProgressCallback,
     7→} from '../types';
     8→
     9→/**
    10→ * Upload encrypted data to IPFS via backend relay.
    11→ * Uses axios for upload progress tracking.
    12→ *
    13→ * Replaces: apps/web/src/lib/api/ipfs.ts addToIpfs()
    14→ * Change: Takes SdkContext instead of reading useAuthStore.getState()
    15→ */
    16→export async function addToIpfs(
    17→  ctx: SdkContext,
    18→  encryptedData: Uint8Array,
    19→  onProgress?: ProgressCallback,
    20→  cancelToken?: CancelToken
    21→): Promise<IpfsAddResult> {
    22→  const token = await ctx.getAccessToken();
    23→  const blob = new Blob([encryptedData as BlobPart]);
    24→  const formData = new FormData();
    25→  formData.append('file', blob);
    26→
    27→  const response = await axios.post<IpfsAddResult>(`${ctx.apiUrl}/ipfs/upload`, formData, {
    28→    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    29→    onUploadProgress: (event: AxiosProgressEvent) => {
    30→      if (event.total && onProgress) {
    31→        onProgress(Math.round((event.loaded * 100) / event.total));
    32→      }
    33→    },
    34→    cancelToken,
    35→  });
    36→  return response.data;
    37→}
    38→
    39→/**
    40→ * Unpin file from IPFS via backend relay.
    41→ *
    42→ * Replaces: apps/web/src/lib/api/ipfs.ts unpinFromIpfs()
    43→ * Change: Takes SdkContext instead of reading useAuthStore.getState()
    44→ */
    45→export async function unpinFromIpfs(ctx: SdkContext, cid: string): Promise<void> {
    46→  const token = await ctx.getAccessToken();
    47→  await axios.post(
    48→    `${ctx.apiUrl}/ipfs/unpin`,
    49→    { cid },
    50→    { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
    51→  );
    52→}
    53→
    54→/**
    55→ * Fetch encrypted data from IPFS via backend relay.
    56→ * Supports download progress tracking.
    57→ *
    58→ * Replaces: apps/web/src/lib/api/ipfs.ts fetchFromIpfs()
    59→ * Change: Takes SdkContext instead of reading useAuthStore.getState()
    60→ */
    61→export async function fetchFromIpfs(
    62→  ctx: SdkContext,
    63→  cid: string,
    64→  onProgress?: DownloadProgressCallback
    65→): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    66→  const token = await ctx.getAccessToken();
    67→  const response = await fetch(`${ctx.apiUrl}/ipfs/${cid}`, {
    68→    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    69→  });
    70→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch from IPFS: ${response.status}`);
    71→
    72→  const contentLength = response.headers.get('Content-Length');
    73→  if (!onProgress || !contentLength) {
    74→    return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  const total = parseInt(contentLength, 10);
    78→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    79→  if (!reader) throw new Error('ReadableStream not supported');
    80→
    81→  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
    82→  let loaded = 0;
    83→  while (true) {
    84→    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    85→    if (done) break;
    86→    chunks.push(value);
    87→    loaded += value.length;
    88→    onProgress(loaded, total);
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  const result = new Uint8Array(loaded);
    92→  let offset = 0;
    93→  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    94→    result.set(chunk, offset);
    95→    offset += chunk.length;
    96→  }
    97→  return result;
    98→}
    99→
