```
     1→/**
     2→ * IPNS Service - Record creation, publishing, and resolution
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracted from: apps/web/src/services/ipns.service.ts
     5→ * Change: Uses @cipherbox/api-client generated functions instead of web app local imports.
     6→ * The ipns.service.ts was already clean (no store deps), so the extraction is straightforward.
     7→ */
     8→
     9→import { createIpnsRecord, marshalIpnsRecord, IPNS_SIGNATURE_PREFIX } from '@cipherbox/core';
    10→import { verifyEd25519, concatBytes } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    11→import {
    12→  ipnsControllerPublishRecord,
    13→  ipnsControllerPublishBatch,
    14→  ipnsControllerResolveRecord,
    15→} from '@cipherbox/api-client';
    16→import type { PublishIpnsEntryDtoRecordType } from '@cipherbox/api-client';
    17→
    18→/**
    19→ * Create an IPNS record locally and publish via backend.
    20→ *
    21→ * The record is signed locally using the Ed25519 private key, then
    22→ * the backend relays it to the IPFS network via delegated routing.
    23→ *
    24→ * @param params.ipnsPrivateKey - Ed25519 private key (64 bytes in libp2p format)
    25→ * @param params.ipnsName - IPNS name (k51.../bafzaa... format)
    26→ * @param params.metadataCid - CID of the encrypted metadata blob
    27→ * @param params.sequenceNumber - Current sequence number (will be incremented before publish)
    28→ * @param params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey - Hex ECIES-wrapped key for TEE (first publish only)
    29→ * @param params.keyEpoch - TEE key epoch (required with encryptedIpnsPrivateKey)
    30→ * @param params.expectedSequenceNumber - Pre-increment sequence number for conflict detection (folder records only)
    31→ */
    32→export async function createAndPublishIpnsRecord(params: {
    33→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    34→  ipnsName: string;
    35→  metadataCid: string;
    36→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
    37→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
    38→  keyEpoch?: number;
    39→  expectedSequenceNumber?: string;
    40→}): Promise<{ success: boolean; sequenceNumber: bigint }> {
    41→  // 1. Create IPNS record pointing to /ipfs/{metadataCid}
    42→  // 24 hour lifetime (will be republished by TEE every 3 hours)
    43→  const record = await createIpnsRecord(
    44→    params.ipnsPrivateKey,
    45→    `/ipfs/${params.metadataCid}`,
    46→    params.sequenceNumber,
    47→    24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 24 hours in ms
    48→  );
    49→
    50→  // 2. Marshal to bytes for transport
    51→  const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
    52→
    53→  // 3. Base64 encode for API transmission (loop-based to avoid call stack overflow on large records)
    54→  let binary = '';
    55→  for (let i = 0; i < recordBytes.length; i++) {
    56→    binary += String.fromCharCode(recordBytes[i]);
    57→  }
    58→  const recordBase64 = btoa(binary);
    59→
    60→  // 4. Call backend API to relay to IPFS network
    61→  const response = await ipnsControllerPublishRecord({
    62→    ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
    63→    record: recordBase64,
    64→    metadataCid: params.metadataCid,
    65→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
    66→    keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
    67→    expectedSequenceNumber: params.expectedSequenceNumber,
    68→  });
    69→
    70→  return {
    71→    success: response.success,
    72→    sequenceNumber: BigInt(response.sequenceNumber),
    73→  };
    74→}
    75→
    76→/**
    77→ * Batch publish multiple IPNS records in a single API call.
    78→ *
    79→ * Sends all records (folder and/or file) to the batch endpoint,
    80→ * which processes them with concurrency-limited parallelism.
    81→ * Partial success is allowed: individual failures do not fail the batch.
    82→ *
    83→ * @param records - Array of IPNS record payloads to publish
    84→ * @returns Success and failure counts
    85→ */
    86→export async function batchPublishIpnsRecords(
    87→  records: Array<{
    88→    ipnsName: string;
    89→    recordBase64: string;
    90→    metadataCid: string;
    91→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
    92→    keyEpoch?: number;
    93→    recordType?: 'folder' | 'file';
    94→    /** Pre-increment sequence number for conflict detection (folder records only) */
    95→    expectedSequenceNumber?: string;
    96→  }>
    97→): Promise<{ totalSucceeded: number; totalFailed: number }> {
    98→  const response = await ipnsControllerPublishBatch({
    99→    records: records.map((r) => ({
   100→      ipnsName: r.ipnsName,
   101→      record: r.recordBase64,
   102→      metadataCid: r.metadataCid,
   103→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: r.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   104→      keyEpoch: r.keyEpoch,
   105→      recordType: r.recordType as PublishIpnsEntryDtoRecordType | undefined,
   106→      expectedSequenceNumber: r.expectedSequenceNumber,
   107→    })),
   108→  });
   109→
   110→  return {
   111→    totalSucceeded: response.totalSucceeded,
   112→    totalFailed: response.totalFailed,
   113→  };
   114→}
   115→
   116→/**
   117→ * Verify the Ed25519 signature on an IPNS record.
   118→ * Per IPFS spec, the signature is over "ipns-signature:" + cborData.
   119→ *
   120→ * @param signatureV2 - base64 Ed25519 signature (64 bytes)
   121→ * @param data - base64 CBOR data that was signed
   122→ * @param pubKey - base64 raw Ed25519 public key (32 bytes)
   123→ * @returns true if valid
   124→ */
   125→export async function verifyIpnsSignature(
   126→  signatureV2: string,
   127→  data: string,
   128→  pubKey: string
   129→): Promise<boolean> {
   130→  const sigBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(signatureV2), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   131→  const dataBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(data), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   132→  const pubKeyBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(pubKey), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   133→
   134→  // Per IPFS IPNS spec, signature is over "ipns-signature:" + cborData
   135→  const signedData = concatBytes(IPNS_SIGNATURE_PREFIX, dataBytes);
   136→  return verifyEd25519(sigBytes, signedData, pubKeyBytes);
   137→}
   138→
   139→/**
   140→ * Resolve an IPNS name to its current CID and sequence number.
   141→ *
   142→ * Calls backend API which relays to the delegated routing service for resolution.
   143→ * When the response includes IPNS signature data (from delegated routing),
   144→ * verifies the Ed25519 signature before trusting the CID.
   145→ *
   146→ * @param ipnsName - IPNS name to resolve (k51.../bafzaa... format)
   147→ * @returns Current CID, sequence number, and signature verification status, or null if not found
   148→ */
   149→export async function resolveIpnsRecord(
   150→  ipnsName: string
   151→): Promise<{ cid: string; sequenceNumber: bigint; signatureVerified: boolean } | null> {
   152→  try {
   153→    const response = await ipnsControllerResolveRecord({ ipnsName });
   154→
   155→    if (!response.success) {
   156→      return null;
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    // Verify IPNS signature if all signature fields are present
   160→    let signatureVerified = false;
   161→    if (response.signatureV2 && response.data && response.pubKey) {
   162→      const valid = await verifyIpnsSignature(response.signatureV2, response.data, response.pubKey);
   163→      if (!valid) {
   164→        throw new Error('IPNS signature verification failed - record may be tampered');
   165→      }
   166→      signatureVerified = true;
   167→    } else {
   168→      console.warn('IPNS resolve returned without signature data, skipping verification');
   169→    }
   170→
   171→    return {
   172→      cid: response.cid,
   173→      sequenceNumber: BigInt(response.sequenceNumber),
   174→      signatureVerified,
   175→    };
   176→  } catch (error) {
   177→    // 404 means IPNS name not found - return null
   178→    // Other errors should propagate (including signature verification failures)
   179→    if (error instanceof Error && (error as Error & { status?: number }).status === 404) {
   180→      return null;
   181→    }
   182→    throw error;
   183→  }
   184→}
   185→

     1→/**
     2→ * Folder operations - Stateless folder CRUD with encryption
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracted from: apps/web/src/services/folder.service.ts
     5→ * All store access replaced with explicit parameters.
     6→ * Functions take metadata in, return updated metadata out.
     7→ */
     8→
     9→import {
    10→  generateEd25519Keypair,
    11→  deriveIpnsName,
    12→  generateRandomBytes,
    13→  wrapKey,
    14→  bytesToHex,
    15→  hexToBytes,
    16→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    17→import {
    18→  encryptFolderMetadata,
    19→  decryptFolderMetadata,
    20→  type FolderMetadata,
    21→  type EncryptedFolderMetadata,
    22→  type FolderEntry,
    23→  type FolderChild,
    24→  type FilePointer,
    25→} from '@cipherbox/core';
    26→import type { SdkContext, TeeKeys } from '../types';
    27→import { addToIpfs, fetchFromIpfs } from '../ipfs';
    28→import { createAndPublishIpnsRecord, resolveIpnsRecord } from '../ipns';
    29→
    30→/**
    31→ * Fetch and decrypt folder metadata from IPFS.
    32→ *
    33→ * @param cid - IPFS CID of the encrypted metadata blob
    34→ * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
    35→ * @param ctx - SDK context for IPFS access
    36→ * @returns Decrypted folder metadata (v2)
    37→ */
    38→export async function fetchAndDecryptMetadata(
    39→  cid: string,
    40→  folderKey: Uint8Array,
    41→  ctx: SdkContext
    42→): Promise<FolderMetadata> {
    43→  const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(ctx, cid);
    44→  const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
    45→  const encrypted: EncryptedFolderMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
    46→  return decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, folderKey);
    47→}
    48→
    49→/**
    50→ * Load a folder's metadata from IPNS.
    51→ *
    52→ * Resolves the folder's IPNS name to get the current metadata CID,
    53→ * fetches and decrypts the metadata.
    54→ *
    55→ * @returns Decrypted folder metadata, sequence number, and CID, or null if IPNS not found
    56→ */
    57→export async function loadFolderMetadata(params: {
    58→  ipnsName: string;
    59→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
    60→  ctx: SdkContext;
    61→}): Promise<{
    62→  metadata: FolderMetadata;
    63→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
    64→  cid: string;
    65→} | null> {
    66→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.ipnsName);
    67→  if (!resolved) return null;
    68→
    69→  const metadata = await fetchAndDecryptMetadata(resolved.cid, params.folderKey, params.ctx);
    70→
    71→  return {
    72→    metadata,
    73→    sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,
    74→    cid: resolved.cid,
    75→  };
    76→}
    77→
    78→/**
    79→ * Create a new subfolder with generated keys.
    80→ *
    81→ * Generates new Ed25519 IPNS keypair and AES-256 folder key,
    82→ * wraps them with the user's public key for storage.
    83→ *
    84→ * @returns Created folder entry and decrypted keys
    85→ */
    86→export async function createSubfolder(params: {
    87→  name: string;
    88→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
    89→  teeKeys?: TeeKeys;
    90→}): Promise<{
    91→  folder: FolderEntry;
    92→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    93→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
    94→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
    95→  keyEpoch?: number;
    96→}> {
    97→  // 1. Generate Ed25519 keypair for folder IPNS
    98→  const ipnsKeypair = await generateEd25519Keypair();
    99→  const ipnsName = await deriveIpnsName(ipnsKeypair.publicKey);
   100→
   101→  // 2. Generate random AES-256 folder key
   102→  const folderKey = generateRandomBytes(32);
   103→
   104→  // 3. Wrap keys with user's public key (ECIES encryption)
   105→  const ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(
   106→    await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, params.userPublicKey)
   107→  );
   108→  const folderKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(await wrapKey(folderKey, params.userPublicKey));
   109→
   110→  // 4. TEE-02: Encrypt IPNS private key with TEE public key for republishing.
   111→  //    Wrap remaining steps in try/catch to zero key material on error.
   112→  //    On success the caller receives the keys, so we only zero on failure.
   113→  try {
   114→    let encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: string | undefined;
   115→    let keyEpoch: number | undefined;
   116→
   117→    if (params.teeKeys?.currentPublicKey) {
   118→      const teePublicKey = hexToBytes(params.teeKeys.currentPublicKey);
   119→      const encryptedKey = await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, teePublicKey);
   120→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey = bytesToHex(encryptedKey);
   121→      keyEpoch = params.teeKeys.currentEpoch;
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    // 5. Create folder entry for parent's metadata
   125→    const now = Date.now();
   126→    const folder: FolderEntry = {
   127→      type: 'folder',
   128→      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
   129→      name: params.name,
   130→      ipnsName,
   131→      ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   132→      folderKeyEncrypted,
   133→      createdAt: now,
   134→      modifiedAt: now,
   135→    };
   136→
   137→    return {
   138→      folder,
   139→      ipnsPrivateKey: ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   140→      folderKey,
   141→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   142→      keyEpoch,
   143→    };
   144→  } catch (error) {
   145→    ipnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
   146→    folderKey.fill(0);
   147→    throw error;
   148→  }
   149→}
   150→
   151→/**
   152→ * Update folder metadata and publish to IPNS.
   153→ *
   154→ * Encrypts the metadata with the folder key, uploads to IPFS,
   155→ * and publishes an updated IPNS record pointing to the new CID.
   156→ *
   157→ * @returns New CID and sequence number
   158→ */
   159→export async function updateFolderMetadataAndPublish(params: {
   160→  children: FolderChild[];
   161→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   162→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   163→  ipnsName: string;
   164→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
   165→  ctx: SdkContext;
   166→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
   167→  keyEpoch?: number;
   168→}): Promise<{ cid: string; newSequenceNumber: bigint }> {
   169→  // 1. Create v2 folder metadata
   170→  const metadata: FolderMetadata = {
   171→    version: 'v2',
   172→    children: params.children,
   173→  };
   174→
   175→  // 2. Encrypt metadata with folder key
   176→  const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
   177→
   178→  // 3. Upload to IPFS via backend relay
   179→  const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
   180→  const encryptedBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
   181→  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes);
   182→
   183→  // 4. Publish IPNS record with conflict retry
   184→  //    On 409, resolve current seq from IPNS and retry once
   185→  let currentSeq = params.sequenceNumber;
   186→
   187→  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
   188→    const newSeq = currentSeq + 1n;
   189→    try {
   190→      await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
   191→        ipnsPrivateKey: params.ipnsPrivateKey,
   192→        ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
   193→        metadataCid: cid,
   194→        sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   195→        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   196→        keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
   197→        expectedSequenceNumber: currentSeq.toString(),
   198→      });
   199→      return { cid, newSequenceNumber: newSeq };
   200→    } catch (err) {
   201→      const is409 =
   202→        (err as Error & { status?: number }).status === 409 ||
   203→        (err as Error & { response?: { status?: number } }).response?.status === 409;
   204→      if (!is409 || attempt > 0) throw err;
   205→
   206→      // Re-sync: resolve current seq from IPNS
   207→      const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.ipnsName);
   208→      if (resolved) {
   209→        currentSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
   210→      } else {
   211→        throw err; // Can't resolve → give up
   212→      }
   213→    }
   214→  }
   215→
   216→  // Should not reach here, but TypeScript needs it
   217→  throw new Error('Publish failed after retry');
   218→}
   219→
   220→/**
   221→ * Rename a child entry (folder or file) in folder metadata.
   222→ *
   223→ * Pure metadata operation: returns updated children array without publishing.
   224→ */
   225→export function renameInFolder(params: {
   226→  children: FolderChild[];
   227→  childId: string;
   228→  newName: string;
   229→}): { updatedChildren: FolderChild[]; renamedChild: FolderChild } {
   230→  const children = [...params.children];
   231→  const index = children.findIndex((c) => c.id === params.childId);
   232→
   233→  if (index === -1) throw new Error('Item not found');
   234→
   235→  const nameExists = children.some((c) => c.name === params.newName && c.id !== params.childId);
   236→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists');
   237→
   238→  const renamedChild = {
   239→    ...children[index],
   240→    name: params.newName,
   241→    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   242→  };
   243→  children[index] = renamedChild;
   244→
   245→  return { updatedChildren: children, renamedChild };
   246→}
   247→
   248→/**
   249→ * Remove a child entry (folder or file) from folder metadata.
   250→ *
   251→ * Pure metadata operation: returns updated children array and the removed item.
   252→ */
   253→export function deleteFromFolder(params: { children: FolderChild[]; childId: string }): {
   254→  updatedChildren: FolderChild[];
   255→  removedItem: FolderChild;
   256→} {
   257→  const index = params.children.findIndex((c) => c.id === params.childId);
   258→  if (index === -1) throw new Error('Item not found');
   259→
   260→  const removedItem = params.children[index];
   261→  const updatedChildren = params.children.filter((c) => c.id !== params.childId);
   262→
   263→  return { updatedChildren, removedItem };
   264→}
   265→
   266→/**
   267→ * Add a file pointer to folder children.
   268→ *
   269→ * Pure metadata operation: returns updated children array with the new file pointer.
   270→ */
   271→export function addFilePointerToFolder(params: {
   272→  children: FolderChild[];
   273→  fileId: string;
   274→  fileName: string;
   275→  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
   276→  ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: string;
   277→}): { updatedChildren: FolderChild[]; filePointer: FilePointer } {
   278→  const nameExists = params.children.some((c) => c.name === params.fileName);
   279→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('A file with this name already exists');
   280→
   281→  const now = Date.now();
   282→  const filePointer: FilePointer = {
   283→    type: 'file',
   284→    id: params.fileId,
   285→    name: params.fileName,
   286→    fileMetaIpnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
   287→    ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: params.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   288→    createdAt: now,
   289→    modifiedAt: now,
   290→  };
   291→
   292→  return {
   293→    updatedChildren: [...params.children, filePointer],
   294→    filePointer,
   295→  };
   296→}
   297→
   298→/**
   299→ * Move a child entry between folders.
   300→ *
   301→ * Pure metadata operation: returns updated source and dest children arrays.
   302→ * Uses add-before-remove pattern conceptually (caller publishes dest first, then source).
   303→ */
   304→export function moveItem(params: {
   305→  sourceChildren: FolderChild[];
   306→  destChildren: FolderChild[];
   307→  childId: string;
   308→}): {
   309→  updatedSourceChildren: FolderChild[];
   310→  updatedDestChildren: FolderChild[];
   311→  movedItem: FolderChild;
   312→} {
   313→  const index = params.sourceChildren.findIndex((c) => c.id === params.childId);
   314→  if (index === -1) throw new Error('Item not found');
   315→
   316→  const movedItem = {
   317→    ...params.sourceChildren[index],
   318→    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   319→  };
   320→
   321→  // Check name collision in destination
   322→  const nameExists = params.destChildren.some((c) => c.name === movedItem.name);
   323→  if (nameExists) {
   324→    throw new Error('An item with this name already exists in destination');
   325→  }
   326→
   327→  const updatedSourceChildren = params.sourceChildren.filter((c) => c.id !== params.childId);
   328→  const updatedDestChildren = [...params.destChildren, movedItem];
   329→
   330→  return { updatedSourceChildren, updatedDestChildren, movedItem };
   331→}
   332→

     1→/**
     2→ * File Metadata Service - Per-file IPNS operations
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracted from: apps/web/src/services/file-metadata.service.ts
     5→ * Change: teeKeys passed explicitly instead of read from useAuthStore.
     6→ * Change: IPFS/IPNS operations use sdk-core internal modules instead of web app imports.
     7→ */
     8→
     9→import {
    10→  generateFileIpnsKeypair,
    11→  encryptFileMetadata,
    12→  decryptFileMetadata,
    13→  createIpnsRecord,
    14→  marshalIpnsRecord,
    15→  type FileMetadata,
    16→  type EncryptedFileMetadata,
    17→  type VersionEntry,
    18→} from '@cipherbox/core';
    19→import { wrapKey, bytesToHex, hexToBytes } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    20→import type { SdkContext, TeeKeys } from '../types';
    21→import { addToIpfs, fetchFromIpfs } from '../ipfs';
    22→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../ipns';
    23→
    24→/** IPNS record lifetime: 24 hours in milliseconds */
    25→const IPNS_LIFETIME_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    26→
    27→/** Maximum number of past versions retained per file (VER-04) */
    28→const MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE = 10;
    29→
    30→/** Safe base64 encoding that avoids call stack overflow from spread operator */
    31→function uint8ToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    32→  let binary = '';
    33→  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
    34→    binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
    35→  }
    36→  return btoa(binary);
    37→}
    38→
    39→/** Record payload ready for batch publish */
    40→export type FileIpnsRecordPayload = {
    41→  ipnsName: string;
    42→  recordBase64: string;
    43→  metadataCid: string;
    44→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
    45→  keyEpoch?: number;
    46→};
    47→
    48→/**
    49→ * Create a per-file IPNS metadata record.
    50→ *
    51→ * Generates a random Ed25519 IPNS keypair for the file, encrypts file metadata
    52→ * with the parent folder's key, uploads to IPFS, and creates a signed IPNS record.
    53→ *
    54→ * @returns File IPNS name, record payload for batch publish, and ECIES-wrapped IPNS private key
    55→ */
    56→export async function createFileMetadata(params: {
    57→  fileId: string;
    58→  cid: string;
    59→  fileKeyEncrypted: string;
    60→  fileIv: string;
    61→  size: number;
    62→  mimeType: string;
    63→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
    64→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
    65→  ctx: SdkContext;
    66→  teeKeys?: TeeKeys;
    67→  encryptionMode?: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
    68→}): Promise<{
    69→  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
    70→  ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
    71→  ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: string;
    72→}> {
    73→  // 1. Generate random Ed25519 IPNS keypair for this file
    74→  const ipnsKeypair = await generateFileIpnsKeypair();
    75→
    76→  // All key-using operations inside try/finally to guarantee zeroization
    77→  try {
    78→    // 2. ECIES-wrap the IPNS private key with user's public key for storage in FilePointer
    79→    const wrappedIpnsKey = await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, params.userPublicKey);
    80→    const ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(wrappedIpnsKey);
    81→
    82→    const now = Date.now();
    83→    const metadata: FileMetadata = {
    84→      version: 'v1',
    85→      cid: params.cid,
    86→      fileKeyEncrypted: params.fileKeyEncrypted,
    87→      fileIv: params.fileIv,
    88→      size: params.size,
    89→      mimeType: params.mimeType,
    90→      encryptionMode: params.encryptionMode ?? 'GCM',
    91→      createdAt: now,
    92→      modifiedAt: now,
    93→    };
    94→
    95→    // 4. Encrypt with parent folderKey
    96→    const encrypted: EncryptedFileMetadata = await encryptFileMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
    97→
    98→    // 5. Upload encrypted metadata to IPFS
    99→    const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
   100→    const encryptedBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
   101→    const { cid: metadataCid } = await addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes);
   102→
   103→    // 6. Create IPNS record (sequence number 1 for new records)
   104→    const record = await createIpnsRecord(
   105→      ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   106→      `/ipfs/${metadataCid}`,
   107→      1n,
   108→      IPNS_LIFETIME_MS
   109→    );
   110→
   111→    // 7. Marshal and base64 encode the record
   112→    const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
   113→    const recordBase64 = uint8ToBase64(recordBytes);
   114→
   115→    // 8. TEE enrollment: encrypt IPNS private key with TEE public key
   116→    let teeEncryptedIpnsPrivateKey: string | undefined;
   117→    let keyEpoch: number | undefined;
   118→
   119→    if (params.teeKeys?.currentPublicKey) {
   120→      const teePublicKey = hexToBytes(params.teeKeys.currentPublicKey);
   121→      const encryptedKey = await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, teePublicKey);
   122→      teeEncryptedIpnsPrivateKey = bytesToHex(encryptedKey);
   123→      keyEpoch = params.teeKeys.currentEpoch;
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    return {
   127→      fileMetaIpnsName: ipnsKeypair.ipnsName,
   128→      ipnsRecord: {
   129→        ipnsName: ipnsKeypair.ipnsName,
   130→        recordBase64,
   131→        metadataCid,
   132→        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: teeEncryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   133→        keyEpoch,
   134→      },
   135→      ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   136→    };
   137→  } finally {
   138→    // Zero the private key on all exit paths (success and failure)
   139→    ipnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
   140→  }
   141→}
   142→
   143→/**
   144→ * Resolve a file's per-IPNS metadata record.
   145→ *
   146→ * Resolves the file's IPNS name to get the current metadata CID,
   147→ * fetches the encrypted metadata from IPFS, and decrypts with the
   148→ * parent folder's key.
   149→ *
   150→ * @returns Decrypted file metadata and the resolved metadata CID
   151→ */
   152→export async function resolveFileMetadata(
   153→  fileMetaIpnsName: string,
   154→  folderKey: Uint8Array,
   155→  ctx: SdkContext
   156→): Promise<{ metadata: FileMetadata; metadataCid: string }> {
   157→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(fileMetaIpnsName);
   158→
   159→  if (!resolved) {
   160→    throw new Error('File metadata IPNS not found');
   161→  }
   162→
   163→  const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(ctx, resolved.cid);
   164→  const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   165→  const encrypted: EncryptedFileMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   166→  const metadata = await decryptFileMetadata(encrypted, folderKey);
   167→
   168→  return { metadata, metadataCid: resolved.cid };
   169→}
   170→
   171→/**
   172→ * Update an existing file's per-IPNS metadata record.
   173→ *
   174→ * Merges updates into the current metadata, re-encrypts, uploads to IPFS,
   175→ * and creates a new IPNS record with an incremented sequence number.
   176→ *
   177→ * @returns Updated IPNS record payload for publish, plus CIDs of pruned versions to unpin
   178→ */
   179→export async function updateFileMetadata(params: {
   180→  fileIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   181→  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
   182→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   183→  currentMetadata: FileMetadata;
   184→  updates: Partial<
   185→    Pick<FileMetadata, 'cid' | 'fileKeyEncrypted' | 'fileIv' | 'size' | 'encryptionMode'>
   186→  >;
   187→  createVersion: boolean;
   188→  ctx: SdkContext;
   189→}): Promise<{
   190→  ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
   191→  prunedCids: string[];
   192→}> {
   193→  // 1. Build version history
   194→  let versions: VersionEntry[] | undefined;
   195→  let prunedCids: string[] = [];
   196→
   197→  if (params.createVersion) {
   198→    const versionEntry: VersionEntry = {
   199→      cid: params.currentMetadata.cid,
   200→      fileKeyEncrypted: params.currentMetadata.fileKeyEncrypted,
   201→      fileIv: params.currentMetadata.fileIv,
   202→      size: params.currentMetadata.size,
   203→      timestamp: Date.now(),
   204→      encryptionMode: params.currentMetadata.encryptionMode ?? 'GCM',
   205→    };
   206→    const allVersions = [versionEntry, ...(params.currentMetadata.versions ?? [])];
   207→
   208→    versions = allVersions.slice(0, MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE);
   209→    prunedCids = allVersions.slice(MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE).map((v) => v.cid);
   210→  } else {
   211→    versions = params.currentMetadata.versions;
   212→  }
   213→
   214→  // 2. Merge updates into current metadata
   215→  const updatedMetadata: FileMetadata = {
   216→    ...params.currentMetadata,
   217→    ...params.updates,
   218→    ...(versions && versions.length > 0 ? { versions } : { versions: undefined }),
   219→    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   220→  };
   221→
   222→  // 3. Resolve current IPNS to get sequence number
   223→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.fileMetaIpnsName);
   224→  if (!resolved) {
   225→    throw new Error(
   226→      `Cannot update file metadata: existing IPNS record not found for ${params.fileMetaIpnsName}`
   227→    );
   228→  }
   229→  const newSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber + 1n;
   230→
   231→  // 4. Encrypt updated metadata with folderKey
   232→  const encrypted = await encryptFileMetadata(updatedMetadata, params.folderKey);
   233→
   234→  // 5. Upload to IPFS
   235→  const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
   236→  const encryptedBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
   237→  const { cid: metadataCid } = await addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes);
   238→
   239→  // 6. Create new IPNS record with incremented sequence number
   240→  const record = await createIpnsRecord(
   241→    params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,
   242→    `/ipfs/${metadataCid}`,
   243→    newSeq,
   244→    IPNS_LIFETIME_MS
   245→  );
   246→
   247→  const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
   248→  const recordBase64 = uint8ToBase64(recordBytes);
   249→
   250→  return {
   251→    ipnsRecord: {
   252→      ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
   253→      recordBase64,
   254→      metadataCid,
   255→    },
   256→    prunedCids,
   257→  };
   258→}
   259→
```

     1→/**
     2→ * Upload operations - Encrypt and upload files to IPFS
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracted from: apps/web/src/services/upload.service.ts + file-crypto.service.ts
     5→ * Change: Takes raw Uint8Array data instead of File objects (no browser DOM deps).
     6→ * Change: Takes explicit SdkContext instead of reading stores.
     7→ * Does NOT handle: multi-file orchestration, upload queue, progress UI, quota checking.
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import {
    11→  generateFileKey,
    12→  generateIv,
    13→  encryptAesGcm,
    14→  wrapKey,
    15→  clearBytes,
    16→  bytesToHex,
    17→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    18→import type { SdkContext, TeeKeys, ProgressCallback } from '../types';
    19→import { addToIpfs } from '../ipfs';
    20→import { createFileMetadata, type FileIpnsRecordPayload } from '../file';
    21→
    22→/**
    23→ * Result of a single file upload operation.
    24→ */
    25→export type UploadResult = {
    26→  /** IPFS CID of the encrypted file content */
    27→  cid: string;
    28→  /** Encrypted file size in bytes */
    29→  encryptedSize: number;
    30→  /** File metadata IPNS name (for FilePointer) */
    31→  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
    32→  /** File IPNS record payload for batch publish */
    33→  ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
    34→  /** ECIES-wrapped IPNS private key (hex) for storage in FilePointer */
    35→  ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: string;
    36→  /**
    37→   * Plaintext file key (AES-256) for post-upload re-wrapping.
    38→   * The caller MUST clear this with clearBytes() after use.
    39→   * Only present when the caller needs to re-wrap for share recipients.
    40→   */
    41→  fileKey: Uint8Array;
    42→};
    43→
    44→/**
    45→ * Encrypt and upload a file to IPFS, creating file metadata.
    46→ *
    47→ * This is the core single-file upload pipeline:
    48→ * 1. Generate file key + IV
    49→ * 2. Encrypt the file content with AES-256-GCM
    50→ * 3. Upload encrypted blob to IPFS
    51→ * 4. Create per-file IPNS metadata record
    52→ *
    53→ * Returns everything needed to add a FilePointer to the parent folder.
    54→ *
    55→ * @param params.data - Raw file content as Uint8Array
    56→ * @param params.fileId - Pre-generated UUID for this file
    57→ * @param params.mimeType - MIME type of the original file
    58→ * @param params.folderKey - Parent folder's decrypted AES-256 key
    59→ * @param params.userPublicKey - User's secp256k1 public key for ECIES wrapping
    60→ * @param params.ctx - SDK context for API access
    61→ * @param params.onProgress - Optional upload progress callback
    62→ * @param params.teeKeys - Optional TEE keys for IPNS private key enrollment
    63→ */
    64→export async function uploadFile(params: {
    65→  data: Uint8Array;
    66→  fileId: string;
    67→  mimeType: string;
    68→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
    69→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
    70→  ctx: SdkContext;
    71→  onProgress?: ProgressCallback;
    72→  teeKeys?: TeeKeys;
    73→}): Promise<UploadResult> {
    74→  // 1. Generate unique file key and IV
    75→  const fileKey = generateFileKey();
    76→  const iv = generateIv();
    77→
    78→  try {
    79→    // 2. Encrypt with AES-256-GCM
    80→    const ciphertext = await encryptAesGcm(params.data, fileKey, iv);
    81→
    82→    // 3. Wrap file key with user's public key (ECIES)
    83→    const wrappedKey = await wrapKey(fileKey, params.userPublicKey);
    84→
    85→    // 4. Upload encrypted content to IPFS
    86→    const { cid, size: encryptedSize } = await addToIpfs(params.ctx, ciphertext, params.onProgress);
    87→
    88→    // 5. Create per-file IPNS metadata record
    89→    const fileMetaResult = await createFileMetadata({
    90→      fileId: params.fileId,
    91→      cid,
    92→      fileKeyEncrypted: bytesToHex(wrappedKey),
    93→      fileIv: bytesToHex(iv),
    94→      size: params.data.length,
    95→      mimeType: params.mimeType,
    96→      folderKey: params.folderKey,
    97→      userPublicKey: params.userPublicKey,
    98→      ctx: params.ctx,
    99→      teeKeys: params.teeKeys,
   100→      encryptionMode: 'GCM',
   101→    });
   102→
   103→    // Return a defensive copy of the file key for re-wrapping.
   104→    // The caller is responsible for clearing it after use.
   105→    const fileKeyCopy = new Uint8Array(fileKey);
   106→
   107→    return {
   108→      cid,
   109→      encryptedSize,
   110→      fileMetaIpnsName: fileMetaResult.fileMetaIpnsName,
   111→      ipnsRecord: fileMetaResult.ipnsRecord,
   112→      ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: fileMetaResult.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   113→      fileKey: fileKeyCopy,
   114→    };
   115→  } finally {
   116→    // 6. Clear the internal copy of the key from memory
   117→    clearBytes(fileKey);
   118→  }
   119→}
   120→

     1→/**
     2→ * SDK E2E Test Harness
     3→ *
     4→ * Creates authenticated CipherBoxClient instances backed by real API accounts.
     5→ * Extracted from packages/sdk/src/__tests__/integration.test.ts pattern.
     6→ *
     7→ * Each call to createTestContext() creates a unique user via /auth/test-login,
     8→ * initializes their vault, and returns a ready-to-use CipherBoxClient.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import { CipherBoxClient } from '@cipherbox/sdk';
    12→import { setApiClientConfig } from '@cipherbox/api-client';
    13→import { initializeVault, encryptVaultKeys } from '@cipherbox/core';
    14→import { deriveIpnsName, hexToBytes, bytesToHex } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    15→
    16→const API_URL = process.env.SDK_E2E_API_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000';
    17→const SECRET = process.env.SDK_E2E_SECRET ?? 'e2e-test-secret-do-not-use-in-production';
    18→const THROTTLE_BYPASS = process.env.THROTTLE_BYPASS_SECRET ?? '';
    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Build default headers for fetch calls.
    22→ * Includes the throttle bypass header when THROTTLE_BYPASS_SECRET is set.
    23→ */
    24→function fetchHeaders(extra: Record<string, string> = {}): Record<string, string> {
    25→  const headers: Record<string, string> = { ...extra };
    26→  if (THROTTLE_BYPASS) {
    27→    headers['X-Throttle-Bypass'] = THROTTLE_BYPASS;
    28→  }
    29→  return headers;
    30→}
    31→
    32→/** Default headers for the api-client axios instance (throttle bypass). */
    33→function axiosDefaultHeaders(): Record<string, string> | undefined {
    34→  return THROTTLE_BYPASS ? { 'X-Throttle-Bypass': THROTTLE_BYPASS } : undefined;
    35→}
    36→
    37→/** Core account data returned by createTestAccount (shared between sdk-e2e and load tests). */
    38→export interface TestAccount {
    39→  client: CipherBoxClient;
    40→  accessToken: string;
    41→  publicKey: Uint8Array;
    42→  privateKey: Uint8Array;
    43→  rootIpnsName: string;
    44→  rootFolderKey: Uint8Array;
    45→  rootIpnsKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array };
    46→  email: string;
    47→}
    48→
    49→export interface TestContext extends TestAccount {
    50→  /** Destroy the client and zero key material */
    51→  cleanup: () => void;
    52→}
    53→
    54→export interface CreateAccountOptions {
    55→  apiUrl?: string;
    56→  secret?: string;
    57→  label: string;
    58→  emailPrefix?: string;
    59→}
    60→
    61→/**
    62→ * Create a test account with an initialized vault and CipherBoxClient.
    63→ * This is the shared core used by both sdk-e2e (createTestContext) and
    64→ * load tests (createPoolClient).
    65→ */
    66→export async function createTestAccount(opts: CreateAccountOptions): Promise<TestAccount> {
    67→  const apiUrl = opts.apiUrl ?? API_URL;
    68→  const secret = opts.secret ?? SECRET;
    69→  const prefix = opts.emailPrefix ?? 'sdk-e2e';
    70→  const email = `${prefix}-${opts.label}-${Date.now()}@example.com`;
    71→
    72→  // 1. Authenticate
    73→  const loginRes = await fetch(`${apiUrl}/auth/test-login`, {
    74→    method: 'POST',
    75→    headers: fetchHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
    76→    body: JSON.stringify({ email, secret }),
    77→  });
    78→  if (!loginRes.ok) {
    79→    throw new Error(`test-login failed (${loginRes.status}): ${await loginRes.text()}`);
    80→  }
    81→  const { accessToken, publicKeyHex, privateKeyHex } = await loginRes.json();
    82→  const publicKey = hexToBytes(publicKeyHex);
    83→  const privateKey = hexToBytes(privateKeyHex);
    84→
    85→  // 2. Initialize vault
    86→  const vault = await initializeVault(privateKey);
    87→  const encrypted = await encryptVaultKeys(vault, publicKey);
    88→  const rootIpnsName = await deriveIpnsName(vault.rootIpnsKeypair.publicKey);
    89→
    90→  // 3. Register vault on server
    91→  const initRes = await fetch(`${apiUrl}/vault/init`, {
    92→    method: 'POST',
    93→    headers: fetchHeaders({
    94→      Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
    95→      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    96→    }),
    97→    body: JSON.stringify({
    98→      ownerPublicKey: bytesToHex(publicKey),
    99→      encryptedRootFolderKey: bytesToHex(encrypted.encryptedRootFolderKey),
   100→      encryptedRootIpnsPrivateKey: bytesToHex(encrypted.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey),
   101→      rootIpnsName,
   102→    }),
   103→  });
   104→  if (!initRes.ok) {
   105→    throw new Error(`vault/init failed (${initRes.status}): ${await initRes.text()}`);
   106→  }
   107→
   108→  // 4. Configure API client singleton (includes throttle bypass header)
   109→  setApiClientConfig({
   110→    baseUrl: apiUrl,
   111→    getAccessToken: async () => accessToken,
   112→    defaultHeaders: axiosDefaultHeaders(),
   113→  });
   114→
   115→  // 5. Create and configure CipherBoxClient
   116→  const client = new CipherBoxClient({
   117→    apiUrl,
   118→    getAccessToken: async () => accessToken,
   119→    vaultKeypair: { publicKey, privateKey },
   120→    rootIpnsName,
   121→    rootFolderKey: vault.rootFolderKey,
   122→  });
   123→  client.registerFolder(rootIpnsName, vault.rootFolderKey, vault.rootIpnsKeypair, [], 0n);
   124→
   125→  return {
   126→    client,
   127→    accessToken,
   128→    publicKey,
   129→    privateKey,
   130→    rootIpnsName,
   131→    rootFolderKey: vault.rootFolderKey,
   132→    rootIpnsKeypair: vault.rootIpnsKeypair,
   133→    email,
   134→  };
   135→}
   136→
   137→/**
   138→ * Create a fully-initialized test context (convenience wrapper over createTestAccount).
   139→ */
   140→export async function createTestContext(label: string): Promise<TestContext> {
   141→  const account = await createTestAccount({ label });
   142→  return { ...account, cleanup: () => account.client.destroy() };
   143→}
   144→
   145→/**
   146→ * Delete a test account (cleanup after tests).
   147→ */
   148→export async function deleteTestAccount(
   149→  ctx: { accessToken: string },
   150→  apiUrl = API_URL
   151→): Promise<void> {
   152→  try {
   153→    const res = await fetch(`${apiUrl}/auth/account`, {
   154→      method: 'DELETE',
   155→      headers: fetchHeaders({
   156→        Authorization: `Bearer ${ctx.accessToken}`,
   157→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   158→      }),
   159→      body: JSON.stringify({ confirmation: 'DELETE' }),
   160→    });
   161→    if (!res.ok) {
   162→      console.warn(`Account deletion failed (${res.status}): ${await res.text()}`);
   163→    }
   164→  } catch (err) {
   165→    console.warn(`Account deletion error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
   166→  }
   167→}
   168→
   169→/**
   170→ * fetch() wrapper that automatically injects the throttle bypass header.
   171→ * Use instead of raw fetch() in test suites to avoid 429s.
   172→ */
   173→export function testFetch(url: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
   174→  const headers = new Headers(init?.headers);
   175→  if (THROTTLE_BYPASS) {
   176→    headers.set('X-Throttle-Bypass', THROTTLE_BYPASS);
   177→  }
   178→  return fetch(url, { ...init, headers });
   179→}
   180→
   181→export { API_URL, SECRET, fetchHeaders };
   182→

     1→/**
     2→ * Multi-Account Test Fixture
     3→ *
     4→ * Creates N test accounts for tests that require interaction between users
     5→ * (sharing, invite links). Handles the setApiClientConfig singleton caveat
     6→ * by switching the active account before each operation.

     8→
     9→import { setApiClientConfig } from '@cipherbox/api-client';
    10→import { createTestContext, deleteTestAccount, type TestContext, API_URL } from './test-harness';
    11→
    12→export interface MultiAccountFixture {
    13→  /** All created test contexts, indexed by label */
    14→  accounts: Map<string, TestContext>;
    15→  /**
    16→   * Switch the api-client singleton to the given account.
    17→   * Must be called before any operation that uses the singleton
    18→   * (e.g., sdk-core IPNS functions).
    19→   */
    20→  switchTo: (label: string) => void;
    21→  /** Destroy all clients and delete all accounts */
    22→  cleanupAll: () => Promise<void>;
    23→}
    24→
    25→/**
    26→ * Create N test accounts with the given labels.
    27→ *
    28→ * @example
    29→ * const fixture = await createMultiAccountFixture(['alice', 'bob']);
    30→ * const alice = fixture.accounts.get('alice')!;
    31→ * const bob = fixture.accounts.get('bob')!;
    32→ */
    33→export async function createMultiAccountFixture(labels: string[]): Promise<MultiAccountFixture> {
    34→  const accounts = new Map<string, TestContext>();
    35→
    36→  // Create accounts sequentially to avoid race conditions on vault init
    37→  for (const label of labels) {
    38→    const ctx = await createTestContext(label);
    39→    accounts.set(label, ctx);
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const switchTo = (label: string): void => {
    43→    const ctx = accounts.get(label);
    44→    if (!ctx) throw new Error(`No account with label "${label}"`);
    45→    const defaultHeaders = process.env.THROTTLE_BYPASS_SECRET
    46→      ? { 'X-Throttle-Bypass': process.env.THROTTLE_BYPASS_SECRET }
    47→      : undefined;
    48→    setApiClientConfig({
    49→      baseUrl: API_URL,
    50→      getAccessToken: async () => ctx.accessToken,
    51→      defaultHeaders,
    52→    });
    53→  };
    54→
    55→  const cleanupAll = async (): Promise<void> => {
    56→    for (const [, ctx] of accounts) {
    57→      ctx.cleanup();
    58→      await deleteTestAccount(ctx);
    59→    }
    60→    accounts.clear();
    61→  };
    62→
    63→  return { accounts, switchTo, cleanupAll };
    64→}
    65→

     1→/**
     2→ * Load Test Client Pool
     3→ *
     4→ * Manages N CipherBoxClient instances, each with its own test account.
     5→ * Creates accounts in parallel, distributes workloads, and collects metrics.
     6→ *
     7→ * Reuses the shared createTestAccount/deleteTestAccount from sdk-e2e
     8→ * to avoid duplicating the 5-step account provisioning sequence.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import { setApiClientConfig } from '@cipherbox/api-client';
    12→import {
    13→  createTestAccount,
    14→  deleteTestAccount,
    15→  type TestAccount,
    16→} from '../../../sdk-e2e/src/fixtures/test-harness';
    17→import { MetricsCollector, type OperationMetrics } from './metrics';
    18→import { printSummary, toJsonReport } from './reporter';
    19→
    20→const API_URL = process.env.LOAD_TEST_API_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000';
    21→const SECRET = process.env.LOAD_TEST_SECRET ?? 'e2e-test-secret-do-not-use-in-production';
    22→
    23→export interface PoolClient extends TestAccount {
    24→  id: number;
    25→  metrics: MetricsCollector;
    26→}
    27→
    28→export interface ClientPoolOptions {
    29→  clientCount: number;
    30→  label: string;
    31→}
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Create a pool of N authenticated CipherBoxClient instances.
    35→ *
    36→ * Each client has its own test account, vault, and metrics collector.
    37→ * The api-client singleton is configured once (all clients share baseUrl,
    38→ * tokens are per-request via getAccessToken closure).
    39→ */
    40→export async function createClientPool(opts: ClientPoolOptions): Promise<PoolClient[]> {
    41→  const { clientCount, label } = opts;
    42→  console.log(`Creating ${clientCount} test accounts for "${label}"...`);
    43→  const start = performance.now();
    44→
    45→  // Create accounts in parallel batches of 5 to avoid overwhelming the API
    46→  const clients: PoolClient[] = [];
    47→  const batchSize = 5;
    48→
    49→  for (let batch = 0; batch < clientCount; batch += batchSize) {
    50→    const batchEnd = Math.min(batch + batchSize, clientCount);
    51→    const promises = [];
    52→
    53→    for (let i = batch; i < batchEnd; i++) {
    54→      promises.push(
    55→        createTestAccount({
    56→          apiUrl: API_URL,
    57→          secret: SECRET,
    58→          label: `${label}-${i}`,
    59→          emailPrefix: 'load',
    60→        }).then((account): PoolClient => ({ ...account, id: i, metrics: new MetricsCollector() }))
    61→      );
    62→    }
    63→
    64→    const results = await Promise.allSettled(promises);
    65→    for (const result of results) {
    66→      if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {
    67→        clients.push(result.value);
    68→      } else {
    69→        console.warn(`Failed to create pool client: ${result.reason}`);
    70→      }
    71→    }
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  // Configure the singleton once — all clients share the same baseUrl
    75→  // Individual tokens are injected via the getAccessToken closure on each client
    76→  if (clients.length > 0) {
    77→    const defaultHeaders = process.env.THROTTLE_BYPASS_SECRET
    78→      ? { 'X-Throttle-Bypass': process.env.THROTTLE_BYPASS_SECRET }
    79→      : undefined;
    80→    setApiClientConfig({
    81→      baseUrl: API_URL,
    82→      getAccessToken: async () => clients[0].accessToken,
    83→      defaultHeaders,
    84→    });
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  const elapsed = performance.now() - start;
    88→  console.log(
    89→    `Created ${clients.length}/${clientCount} clients in ${(elapsed / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`
    90→  );
    91→
    92→  return clients;
    93→}
    94→
    95→/**
    96→ * Destroy all clients in the pool and delete test accounts.
    97→ */
    98→export async function destroyClientPool(pool: PoolClient[]): Promise<void> {
    99→  console.log(`Cleaning up ${pool.length} test accounts...`);
   100→  for (const pc of pool) {
   101→    pc.client.destroy();
   102→    await deleteTestAccount(pc, API_URL);
   103→  }
   104→}
   105→
   106→/**
   107→ * Aggregate metrics from all pool clients, print summary, and return metrics.
   108→ * Extracts the repeated boilerplate from every load test scenario.
   109→ */
   110→export async function aggregateAndReport(
   111→  scenarioName: string,
   112→  pool: PoolClient[]
   113→): Promise<OperationMetrics[]> {
   114→  const allMetrics = new MetricsCollector();
   115→
   116→  let minTimestamp = Infinity;
   117→  let maxTimestamp = 0;
   118→
   119→  for (const pc of pool) {
   120→    for (const sample of pc.metrics.getReadonlySamples()) {
   121→      allMetrics.record(sample);
   122→      if (sample.timestamp < minTimestamp) minTimestamp = sample.timestamp;
   123→      if (sample.timestamp > maxTimestamp) maxTimestamp = sample.timestamp;
   124→    }
   125→  }
   126→
   127→  // Set collector timing from sample timestamps for accurate throughput calculation
   128→  allMetrics.setElapsedMs(maxTimestamp > minTimestamp ? maxTimestamp - minTimestamp : 1);
   129→
   130→  const totalDuration = maxTimestamp > minTimestamp ? maxTimestamp - minTimestamp : 0;
   131→  const metrics = allMetrics.getMetrics();
   132→
   133→  printSummary(scenarioName, metrics, totalDuration, pool.length);
   134→
   135→  // Write JSON report to file for CI artifact upload
   136→  const reportJson = toJsonReport(scenarioName, metrics, totalDuration, pool.length);
   137→  console.log('\nJSON Report:\n' + reportJson);
   138→
   139→  try {
   140→    const fs = await import('node:fs/promises');
   141→    const slug = scenarioName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-');
   142→    await fs.writeFile(`metrics-${slug}.json`, reportJson);
   143→  } catch {
   144→    // Non-fatal — CI artifact upload will just find no files
   145→  }
   146→
   147→  return metrics;
   148→}
   149→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Download operations - Fetch and decrypt files from IPFS
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracted from: apps/web/src/services/download.service.ts
     5→ * Change: Takes SdkContext for IPFS access.
     6→ * Does NOT handle: browser download trigger (document.createElement, URL.createObjectURL).
     7→ */
     8→
     9→import { decryptAesGcm, decryptAesCtr, unwrapKey, hexToBytes, clearBytes } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    10→import type { SdkContext, DownloadProgressCallback } from '../types';
    11→import { fetchFromIpfs } from '../ipfs';
    12→
    13→/**
    14→ * Download and decrypt a file from IPFS.
    15→ *
    16→ * Fetches the encrypted file content from IPFS via the backend relay,
    17→ * unwraps the file key, and decrypts the content.
    18→ *
    19→ * @param params.cid - IPFS CID of the encrypted file content
    20→ * @param params.fileKeyEncrypted - Hex-encoded ECIES-wrapped file key
    21→ * @param params.fileIv - Hex-encoded IV used for encryption
    22→ * @param params.userPrivateKey - User's secp256k1 private key for key unwrapping
    23→ * @param params.encryptionMode - Encryption mode: 'GCM' (default) or 'CTR'
    24→ * @param params.ctx - SDK context for IPFS access
    25→ * @param params.onProgress - Optional download progress callback
    26→ * @returns Decrypted file content as Uint8Array
    27→ */
    28→export async function downloadAndDecrypt(params: {
    29→  cid: string;
    30→  fileKeyEncrypted: string;
    31→  fileIv: string;
    32→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    33→  encryptionMode?: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
    34→  ctx: SdkContext;
    35→  onProgress?: DownloadProgressCallback;
    36→}): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    37→  // 1. Fetch encrypted file from IPFS
    38→  const ciphertext = await fetchFromIpfs(params.ctx, params.cid, params.onProgress);
    39→
    40→  // 2. Convert hex strings to bytes
    41→  const iv = hexToBytes(params.fileIv);
    42→  const wrappedKey = hexToBytes(params.fileKeyEncrypted);
    43→
    44→  // 3. Unwrap file key using user's private key
    45→  const fileKey = await unwrapKey(wrappedKey, params.userPrivateKey);
    46→
    47→  try {
    48→    // 4. Decrypt file content (CTR for streaming media, GCM for everything else)
    49→    const plaintext =
    50→      params.encryptionMode === 'CTR'
    51→        ? await decryptAesCtr(ciphertext, fileKey, iv)
    52→        : await decryptAesGcm(ciphertext, fileKey, iv);
    53→    return plaintext;
    54→  } finally {
    55→    // 5. Clear file key from memory
    56→    clearBytes(fileKey);
    57→  }
    58→}
    59→
```

  "old_string": "export const customInstance = async <T>(\n  config: AxiosRequestConfig,\n  options?: AxiosRequestConfig\n): Promise<T> => {\n  const clientConfig = getApiClientConfig();\n  const token = await clientConfig.getAccessToken();\n  const instance = getCachedInstance();\n\n  const response = await instance.request<T>({\n    ...config,\n    ...options,\n    headers: {\n      ...config.headers,\n      ...options?.headers,\n      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),\n    },\n  });\n\n  return response.data;\n};",
  "new_string": "export const customInstance = async <T>(\n  config: AxiosRequestConfig,\n  options?: AxiosRequestConfig & { _axiosInstance?: AxiosInstance }\n): Promise<T> => {\n  const { _axiosInstance, ...restOptions } = options ?? {};\n\n  if (_axiosInstance) {\n    // Instance-scoped path: the provided instance has its own interceptors for auth\n    const response = await _axiosInstance.request<T>({\n      ...config,\n      ...restOptions,\n      headers: {\n        ...config.headers,\n        ...restOptions.headers,\n      },\n    });\n    return response.data;\n  }\n\n  // Singleton path: existing behavior\n  const clientConfig = getApiClientConfig();\n  const token = await clientConfig.getAccessToken();\n  const instance = getCachedInstance();\n\n  const response = await instance.request<T>({\n    ...config,\n    ...restOptions,\n    headers: {\n      ...config.headers,\n      ...restOptions.headers,\n      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),\n    },\n  });\n\n  return response.data;\n};"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/sdk-e2e/src/fixtures/multi-account.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Multi-Account Test Fixture\n *\n * Creates N test accounts for tests that require interaction between users\n * (sharing, invite links). Each CipherBoxClient owns its own axios instance,\n * so no singleton switching is needed.\n */\n\nimport { createTestContext, deleteTestAccount, type TestContext } from './test-harness';\n\nexport interface MultiAccountFixture {\n  /** All created test contexts, indexed by label */\n  accounts: Map<string, TestContext>;\n  /** Destroy all clients and delete all accounts */\n  cleanupAll: () => Promise<void>;\n}\n\n/**\n * Create N test accounts with the given labels.\n *\n * @example\n * const fixture = await createMultiAccountFixture(['alice', 'bob']);\n * const alice = fixture.accounts.get('alice')!;\n * const bob = fixture.accounts.get('bob')!;\n */\nexport async function createMultiAccountFixture(labels: string[]): Promise<MultiAccountFixture> {\n  const accounts = new Map<string, TestContext>();\n\n  // Create accounts sequentially to avoid race conditions on vault init\n  for (const label of labels) {\n    const ctx = await createTestContext(label);\n    accounts.set(label, ctx);\n  }\n\n  const cleanupAll = async (): Promise<void> => {\n    for (const [, ctx] of accounts) {\n      ctx.cleanup();\n      await deleteTestAccount(ctx);\n    }\n    accounts.clear();\n  };\n\n  return { accounts, cleanupAll };\n}\n"
}
```

    60→ * Load and decrypt bin metadata from IPNS.

    62→async function loadBinMetadataInternal(params: {
    63→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    64→  ctx: SdkContext;
    65→}): Promise<{
    66→  metadata: RecycleBinMetadata;
    67→  ipnsName: string;

    71→  const resolved = await sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord(binIpns.ipnsName);
    72→
    73→  if (!resolved) return null;
    74→
    75→  const encryptedBytes = await sdkCore.fetchFromIpfs(params.ctx, resolved.cid);
    76→  const metadata = await decryptBinMetadata(encryptedBytes, params.userPrivateKey);
    77→

    79→    metadata,
    80→    ipnsName: binIpns.ipnsName,

    88→async function saveBinMetadata(params: {

   106→## `setApiClientConfig` Singleton Caveat
   107→
   108→`@cipherbox/api-client` has a module-level singleton for the axios instance. This creates a constraint for multi-account tests:
   109→
   110→- **Single-account tests**: Unaffected — each `CipherBoxClient` uses its own `getAccessToken` closure
   111→- **Multi-account tests** (sharing, invites): Must call `fixture.switchTo(label)` before operations that flow through `sdk-core` (IPNS publish/resolve uses the singleton)
   112→- **Cross-suite contamination**: If a multi-account suite runs before a single-account suite, the singleton may point to the wrong account. Re-set it with `setApiClientConfig()` when needed.
   113→
   114→## Why Node.js for load tests (not k6)
   115→
   116→k6 uses a custom Go-based JS runtime that doesn't support the full Node.js crypto ecosystem (Web Crypto API, `@noble/*`, `eciesjs`). The SDK packages are pure TypeScript/ESM. Porting them to k6 would require significant effort. Node.js with concurrent `CipherBoxClient` instances and `Promise.allSettled` is the natural fit — the bottleneck is network I/O, not CPU.
   117→

     1→---
     2→name: SDK Testing & Load Testing Strategy
     3→description: Architecture decisions for SDK-driven E2E tests, load tests, rate limiting bypass, and horizontal scaling test strategy
     4→type: project
     5→---
     6→
     7→## SDK-Driven Test Suites (PR #318)
     8→
     9→Two new test packages replace browser-dependent testing for programmatic verification:
    10→
    11→- `tests/sdk-e2e/` (`@cipherbox/sdk-e2e`) — 10 suites, 83 tests, ~11s against local API
    12→- `tests/load/` (`@cipherbox/load-tests`) — 5 load scenarios with metrics collection
    13→
    14→Both drive `CipherBoxClient` directly against the API — no Playwright, no browser, no UI selectors.
    15→
    16→**Why:** Playwright E2E tests suffer from inherent browser-automation flakiness (timing, selectors, WebSocket disconnects). SDK tests verify the same operations 5-10x faster with deterministic behavior.
    17→
    18→**How to apply:** SDK E2E tests are the primary correctness gate on PRs (via `sdk-e2e.yml`). Playwright E2E tests remain for UI-specific verification. Load tests are manual-dispatch only (`load-test.yml`).
    19→
    20→## Rate Limiting & Test Environments
    21→
    22→**Problem:** The API has global throttling (10 req/s, 100 req/min in production). SDK tests generate 100+ requests per suite — a single test file exceeds the minute limit.
    23→
    24→**Current solution (2026-03-22):** `NODE_ENV=test` relaxes limits to 200 req/s and 10k req/min in `apps/api/src/app.module.ts`. This works for local dev and CI (where the API runs in test mode).
    25→
    26→**Staging problem:** Staging runs `NODE_ENV=production` with production throttle limits. Load tests targeting staging will hit 429s.
    27→
    28→**Decided approach:** Implement a throttle-bypass header (`X-Throttle-Bypass`) verified against a secret (`THROTTLE_BYPASS_SECRET`). Test harnesses inject the header; production never sets the secret so the header is a no-op.
    29→
    30→**Why not just relax staging limits:** The throttler serves two purposes — abuse protection AND backpressure signaling. Removing it entirely means load tests can't detect when the system is genuinely overloaded.
    31→
    32→## Horizontal Scaling & Load Test Strategy
    33→
    34→**Key insight:** Staging (single node) and production (horizontally scaled) answer different questions:
    35→
    36→| Environment | Question answered | Throttle behavior |
    37→|------------|-------------------|-------------------|
    38→| **Local/CI** | "Do SDK operations work correctly?" | Bypassed (NODE_ENV=test) |
    39→| **Staging** | "Does a single node handle its expected per-node share of traffic?" | Bypassed via secret header for throughput tests; real limits for throttle validation tests |
    40→| **Production** | "Does the cluster handle peak aggregate traffic?" | Real limits; distribute load across multiple source IPs |
    41→
    42→**Future work when prod scales horizontally:**
    43→- Add a "throttle validation" load test scenario that does NOT bypass the throttler — verifies the throttler itself behaves correctly under load
    44→- Production load tests should distribute across multiple source IPs (or use per-user throttle keying) to simulate real multi-client load against the cluster
    45→- The bypass header approach scales — each environment controls its own secret; CI and staging can bypass while production doesn't set the secret
    46→
    47→## Running SDK E2E Tests Locally
    48→
    49→**Prerequisites:** PostgreSQL, IPFS (Kubo), Redis on host (192.168.133.114 or localhost), mock-ipns-routing
    50→
    51→```bash
    52→# Start services
    53→node tools/mock-ipns-routing/dist/index.js &
    54→NODE_ENV=test pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api dev &
    55→
    56→# Run tests
    57→SDK_E2E_API_URL=http://localhost:3000 \
    58→SDK_E2E_SECRET=[REDACTED] \
    59→pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk-e2e test
    60→```
    61→
    62→**Gotcha:** The API `.env` file must have `NODE_ENV=test` for relaxed throttle limits. The env var passed to the shell may be overridden by `.env` file loaded by NestJS ConfigModule.
    63→
    64→## setApiClientConfig Singleton Caveat
    65→
    66→`@cipherbox/api-client` uses a module-level singleton for the axios instance. Multi-account tests (sharing, invite links) must call `setApiClientConfig()` to switch the active account before each operation that flows through `sdk-core` (IPNS publish/resolve). Single-account tests are unaffected since each `CipherBoxClient` has its own `getAccessToken` closure.
    67→
    68→**Symptom when broken:** 401 errors in tests that run after multi-account test suites — the singleton still points to a different account's token.
    69→
    70→**Fix pattern:** Re-set the singleton at the start of any test that relies on it after multi-account suites may have run.
    71→
```

> AGENT

Here's a summary of what was done:

## Refactor: Instance-scoped axios per CipherBoxClient

**17 files changed** across 6 packages. The module-level singleton that all API calls shared is now bypassed when `CipherBoxClient` creates its own axios instance.

### Core changes
| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `api-client/instance.ts` | `customInstance` accepts optional `_axiosInstance` in options, bypassing singleton |
| `sdk-core/types.ts` | `SdkContext` gains optional `axiosInstance` field |
| `sdk-core/ipns/index.ts` | `createAndPublishIpnsRecord`, `batchPublishIpnsRecords`, `resolveIpnsRecord` accept optional `ctx` to forward instance |
| `sdk-core/folder/index.ts` | Threads ctx to IPNS calls in `loadFolderMetadata` and `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` |
| `sdk-core/file/index.ts` | Threads ctx to IPNS calls in `resolveFileMetadata` and `updateFileMetadata` |
| `sdk/src/types.ts` | `CipherBoxClientConfig` gains optional `defaultHeaders` |
| `sdk/src/client.ts` | Constructor creates own axios instance via `createAxiosInstance()` |
| `sdk/src/bin/index.ts` | Threads ctx to IPNS calls |

### Test infrastructure simplification
- **Removed** `switchTo()` from `MultiAccountFixture` (no longer needed)
- **Removed** all `fixture.switchTo()` calls from `share-operations.test.ts` and `invite-link.test.ts`
- **Removed** `setApiClientConfig` from test harness, client pool, error-cases test, and integration test
- **Updated** `TESTING_STRATEGY.md` to document the new architecture

### Backward compatibility
`setApiClientConfig()` and the singleton still work for the web app (single-user browser context). The instance-scoped path is opt-in via `SdkContext.axiosInstance`.

> DEVELOPER

If I am understanding this correctly the backward compatibility is necessary so that a component in the web app always gets the singleton instance that is available throughtout the webapp?

> AGENT

Yes, exactly. The web app calls generated API functions directly from React components, hooks, and stores — not through `CipherBoxClient`. For example:

- `authControllerRefresh()` in auth logic
- `vaultControllerInitializeVault()` during onboarding
- `sharesControllerCreate()` from the share dialog
- `ipnsControllerResolveRecord()` from folder navigation hooks

These generated functions all go through `customInstance` → singleton. There's no `SdkContext` or `CipherBoxClient` in that call path — they're imported and called directly:

```tsx
// Typical web app usage — no client instance available
import { sharesControllerCreate } from '@cipherbox/api-client';

const res = await sharesControllerCreate({ recipientPublicKey, ... });
```

The singleton gives them a pre-configured axios with baseURL, auth token injection, and 401 refresh — available globally without threading an instance through React's component tree or prop drilling.

The roadmap has this covered: Phase 19.1-05 ("Rewire web app hooks and stores to use SDK") would migrate these direct calls to go through `CipherBoxClient`, at which point the singleton becomes truly unnecessary. Until then, both paths coexist.

> AGENT

So the web app uses **both** paths:
- 8 files use `CipherBoxClient` (folder CRUD, bin, file ops — the rewired parts)
- 13 files still import directly from `@cipherbox/api-client` (auth, vault init, health, shares, invites, device approval, IPFS, IPNS)

You're right that Phase 19.1 was completed — the SDK exists and the web app was partially rewired. But the rewiring was scoped to folder/file/bin operations (the core CRUD). The remaining direct API client imports are for operations that don't go through `CipherBoxClient` yet: auth, vault setup, shares API, device approval, health checks, etc.

So the singleton is still needed for those 13 files. The question is whether it's worth keeping or whether we should finish the migration and eliminate it entirely. Given the scope of this PR is SDK E2E and load testing, I'd say the backward compat is the right call for now — removing the singleton from the web app would be a separate effort.

     2→ * Early API client configuration.
     3→ *
     4→ * Imported at module scope (before any auth API calls) so that
     5→ * @cipherbox/api-client's customInstance is ready when authApi.*

    10→ * onRefreshFailure clears all user stores (same behaviour as the old client.ts interceptor).
    11→ */
    12→import { setApiClientConfig, authControllerRefresh } from '@cipherbox/api-client';
    13→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
    14→
    15→const apiUrl =
    16→  import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ||
    17→  (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? `${window.location.origin}/api` : '/api');
    18→
    19→setApiClientConfig({
    20→  baseUrl: apiUrl,
    21→  getAccessToken: async () => useAuthStore.getState().accessToken || '',
    22→  withCredentials: true,
    23→  refreshAccessToken: async () => {
    24→    const response = await authControllerRefresh({});
    25→    const newToken = response.accessToken;
    26→    useAuthStore.getState().setAccessToken(newToken);
    27→    return newToken;
    28→  },
    29→  onRefreshFailure: () => {
    30→    // Dynamic import to avoid circular dependency at module load time
    31→    import('../lib/clear-user-stores')
    32→      .then(({ clearAllUserStores }) => {
    33→        clearAllUserStores();
    34→      })
    35→      .catch(() => {
    36→        // Chunk load failed — force reload to clear stale session state
    37→        window.location.reload();
    38→      });
    39→  },

```
     1→/**
     2→ * SDK Provider - CipherBoxClient lifecycle management
     3→ *
     4→ * Manages a singleton CipherBoxClient instance for the web app.
     5→ * Created after vault load (login complete), destroyed on logout.
     6→ *
     7→ * The SDK client is the single source of truth for all file operations.
     8→ * Hooks call client methods, and stores subscribe to client events.
     9→ */
    10→import { CipherBoxClient, type CipherBoxClientConfig } from '@cipherbox/sdk';
    11→import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
    12→
    13→let _client: CipherBoxClient | null = null;
    14→
    15→/**
    16→ * Initialize the SDK client. Called after vault is loaded (login complete).
    17→ * The client persists for the session and is destroyed on logout.
    18→ *
    19→ * If a client already exists (e.g., session restoration), it is destroyed
    20→ * before creating the new one.
    21→ */
    22→export function initSdkClient(config: CipherBoxClientConfig): CipherBoxClient {
    23→  if (_client) {
    24→    _client.destroy();
    25→  }
    26→  _client = new CipherBoxClient(config);
    27→  return _client;
    28→}
    29→
    30→/**
    31→ * Get the current SDK client instance.
    32→ * Throws if called before initSdkClient() (i.e., before login).
    33→ */
    34→export function getSdkClient(): CipherBoxClient {
    35→  if (!_client) {
    36→    throw new Error('SDK client not initialized. Call initSdkClient() after login.');
    37→  }
    38→  return _client;
    39→}
    40→
    41→/**
    42→ * Check if the SDK client is initialized (for conditional usage).
    43→ */
    44→export function hasSdkClient(): boolean {
    45→  return _client !== null;
    46→}
    47→
    48→/**
    49→ * Destroy the SDK client. Called on logout.
    50→ * Clears internal state, key caches, and event subscriptions.
    51→ */
    52→export function destroySdkClient(): void {
    53→  if (_client) {
    54→    _client.destroy();
    55→    _client = null;
    56→  }
    57→}
    58→
    59→/**
    60→ * Ensure a folder from the Zustand store is registered in the SDK's
    61→ * internal FolderTree. This bridges the gap between folder navigation
    62→ * (which loads folders into Zustand) and SDK operations (which require
    63→ * folders in the SDK's internal state).
    64→ *
    65→ * Only registers if the SDK doesn't already have the folder. The SDK's
    66→ * internal state (sequence number, children, keys) is authoritative once
    67→ * a folder is registered — all mutations go through the SDK.
    68→ */
    69→export function ensureFolderRegistered(folder: FolderNode): void {
    70→  const client = getSdkClient();
    71→
    72→  // Don't overwrite if SDK already has this folder — its internal state
    73→  // is authoritative (correct sequence number, keys, children from mutations)
    74→  if (client.hasFolder(folder.ipnsName)) return;
    75→
    76→  // Guard: don't register placeholder folders with empty key material —
    77→  // would cause crypto/IPNS errors on subsequent mutations
    78→  if (folder.folderKey.length === 0 || folder.ipnsPrivateKey.length === 0) {
    79→    return;
    80→  }
    81→
    82→  client.registerFolder(
    83→    folder.ipnsName,
    84→    folder.folderKey,
    85→    {
    86→      publicKey: new Uint8Array(0), // Public key derived from private key when needed
    87→      privateKey: folder.ipnsPrivateKey,
    88→    },
    89→    folder.children,
    90→    folder.sequenceNumber
    91→  );
    92→}
    93→

### **Key Observations**

1. **Centralized Configuration**: `/apps/web/src/lib/api-config.ts` is imported at module scope in `/apps/web/src/main.tsx` (line 5) and **must run before any API calls**.

2. **Adapter Pattern**: Most files wrap generated functions in service adapters (e.g., `authApi`, `vaultApi`, `ipnsService`) for consistency and easier testing.

3. **Timing Pattern**:
   - **BEFORE login**: `authControllerLogin`, `authControllerRefresh` (interceptor), identity controller functions, `invitesControllerGetInviteStatus` (public)
   - **AFTER login**: All vault, IPFS, IPNS, shares, device approval, and invite claiming operations
   - **Module load**: `setApiClientConfig` (configures interceptor for refresh token + error handling)

4. **No Direct Imports in Components**: Most components use service wrappers rather than calling generated functions directly. Exception: `ShareDialog.tsx` and `VaultExport.tsx` call some functions directly.

5. **Zero Sensitive Keys**: All re-wrapping operations (shares, invites) properly zero plaintext keys in `finally` blocks.

6. **Axios Integration**: Upload/download progress and cancellation use `AxiosProgressEvent` and `CancelToken` from the client.

---

> AGENT

Good call. The real issue is that CipherBoxClient creates its own instance while the web app services use the singleton — two instances, potentially divergent. The fix is to make them share ONE instance.

Here's the approach:
1. Enhance `createAxiosInstance` with 401 refresh support
2. `api-config.ts` creates the full instance and passes it into `setApiClientConfig`
3. CipherBoxClient accepts an external instance instead of always creating its own
4. `sdk-provider.ts` passes the shared instance to CipherBoxClient

This way both paths use the exact same axios instance — no dual path.
