   145→          DB_USERNAME: postgres
   146→          DB_PASSWORD: postgres
   147→          DB_DATABASE: cipherbox_test
   148→          NODE_ENV: test
   149→          IPFS_PROVIDER: local
   150→          IPFS_LOCAL_API_URL: http://localhost:5001
   151→          IPFS_LOCAL_GATEWAY_URL: http://localhost:8080
   152→
   153→      - name: Format generated files
   154→        run: pnpm prettier --write packages/api-client/openapi.json
   155→
   156→      - name: Verify no uncommitted changes to generated files
   157→        run: |
   158→          git diff --exit-code packages/api-client/openapi.json packages/api-client/src/generated/ packages/api-client/src/models/ \
   159→            || (echo "Generated API client is out of date. Run 'pnpm api:generate' and commit changes." && exit 1)
   160→
   161→  migration-check:
   162→    name: Migration Drift Check
   163→    needs: [changes]
   164→    if: |
   165→      needs.changes.outputs.src == 'true'
   166→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   167→    services:
   168→      postgres:
   169→        image: postgres:16-alpine
   170→        env:
   171→          POSTGRES_USER: postgres
   172→          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
   173→          POSTGRES_DB: cipherbox_test
   174→        ports:
   175→          - 5432:5432
   176→        options: >-
   177→          --health-cmd pg_isready
   178→          --health-interval 10s
   179→          --health-timeout 5s
   180→          --health-retries 5
   181→    steps:
   182→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
   183→
   184→      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
   185→        with:
   186→          version: 10
   187→
   188→      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
   189→        with:
   190→          node-version: '22'
   191→          cache: 'pnpm'
   192→
   193→      - name: Install dependencies
   194→        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
   195→
   196→      - name: Run existing migrations
   197→        run: pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api migration:run
   198→        env:
   199→          DB_HOST: localhost
   200→          DB_PORT: 5432
   201→          DB_USERNAME: postgres
   202→          DB_PASSWORD: postgres
   203→          DB_DATABASE: cipherbox_test
   204→          NODE_ENV: test
   205→
   206→      - name: Check for entity/migration drift
   207→        working-directory: apps/api
   208→        run: |
   209→          set +e
   210→          OUTPUT="$(pnpm typeorm migration:generate src/migrations/DriftCheck -d src/data-source.ts 2>&1)"
   211→          STATUS=$?
   212→          set -e
   213→
   214→          # Check if migration:generate failed before inspecting output files
   215→          if [ "$STATUS" -ne 0 ]; then
   216→            if echo "$OUTPUT" | grep -q "No changes in database schema were found"; then
   217→              echo "No entity/migration drift detected."
   218→              exit 0
   219→            fi
   220→            echo "::error::migration:generate failed unexpectedly."
   221→            echo "$OUTPUT"
   222→            exit 1
   223→          fi
   224→
   225→          if ls src/migrations/*DriftCheck*.ts 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then
   226→            # Our hand-written migrations use explicit constraint/index names, but TypeORM
   227→            # expects auto-generated hash-based names. Filter out cosmetic renames and only
   228→            # fail on real structural drift (new/dropped tables, added/removed columns, type changes).
   229→            if grep -qE 'CREATE TABLE|DROP TABLE|ADD COLUMN|DROP COLUMN|ALTER.*TYPE|RENAME COLUMN' src/migrations/*DriftCheck*.ts; then
   230→              echo "::error::Entity/migration drift detected! Entities define schema changes not covered by migrations."
   231→              echo ""
   232→              echo "Generated migration contents:"
   233→              cat src/migrations/*DriftCheck*.ts
   234→              echo ""
   235→              echo "Fix: Run 'pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api typeorm migration:generate src/migrations/<Name> -d src/data-source.ts' and commit the result."
   236→              exit 1
   237→            else
   238→              echo "Only cosmetic constraint/index naming differences detected (not structural drift). OK."
   239→            fi
   240→          else
   241→            echo "No entity/migration drift detected."
   242→          fi
   243→        env:
   244→          DB_HOST: localhost
   245→          DB_PORT: 5432
   246→          DB_USERNAME: postgres
   247→          DB_PASSWORD: postgres
   248→          DB_DATABASE: cipherbox_test
   249→          NODE_ENV: test
   250→
   251→  test:
   252→    name: Test
   253→    needs: [changes, lint, typecheck]
   254→    if: |
   255→      !failure() && !cancelled() &&
   256→      needs.changes.outputs.src == 'true'
   257→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   258→    services:
   259→      postgres:
   260→        image: postgres:16-alpine
   261→        env:
   262→          POSTGRES_USER: postgres
   263→          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
   264→          POSTGRES_DB: cipherbox_test
   265→        ports:
   266→          - 5432:5432
   267→        options: >-
   268→          --health-cmd pg_isready
   269→          --health-interval 10s
   270→          --health-timeout 5s
   271→          --health-retries 5
   272→      ipfs:
   273→        image: ipfs/kubo:v0.34.0
   274→        ports:
   275→          - 5001:5001
   276→          - 8080:8080
   277→        options: >-
   278→          --health-cmd "ipfs id"
   279→          --health-interval 10s
   280→          --health-timeout 5s
   281→          --health-retries 10
   282→          --health-start-period 30s
   283→    steps:
   284→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
   285→
   286→      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
   287→        with:
   288→          version: 10
   289→
   290→      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
   291→        with:
   292→          node-version: '22'
   293→          cache: 'pnpm'
   294→
   295→      - name: Install dependencies
   296→        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
   297→
   298→      - name: Build packages (needed for cross-package imports in tests)
   299→        run: |
   300→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/crypto build
   301→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/core build
   302→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api-client build
   303→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk-core build
   304→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk build
   305→
   306→      - name: Run API tests with coverage

   308→        env:

   312→          DB_PASSWORD: postgres
   313→          DB_DATABASE: cipherbox_test
   314→          NODE_ENV: test
   315→          IPFS_PROVIDER: local
   316→          IPFS_LOCAL_API_URL: http://localhost:5001
   317→          IPFS_LOCAL_GATEWAY_URL: http://localhost:8080
   318→
   319→      - name: Run Crypto tests with coverage
   320→        run: pnpm --filter @cipherbox/crypto test:coverage
   321→
   322→      - name: Run Core tests with coverage
   323→        run: pnpm --filter @cipherbox/core test:coverage
   324→
   325→      - name: Run SDK-Core tests with coverage
   326→        run: pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk-core test:coverage
   327→
   328→      - name: Run SDK tests with coverage
   329→        run: pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk test:coverage
   330→
   331→      - name: Run API Client tests with coverage

   338→          files: ./apps/api/coverage/lcov.info,./packages/crypto/coverage/lcov.info,./packages/core/coverage/lcov.info,./packages/sdk-core/coverage/lcov.info,./packages/sdk/coverage/lcov.info,./packages/api-client/coverage/lcov.info
   339→          flags: api,crypto,core,sdk-core,sdk,api-client
   340→          fail_ci_if_error: false
   341→

   355→  build:
   356→    name: Build
   357→    needs: [changes, lint, typecheck, api-spec, test, migration-check]
   358→    if: |
   359→      !failure() && !cancelled() &&
   360→      needs.changes.outputs.src == 'true'
   361→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   362→    steps:
   363→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
   364→
   365→      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
   366→        with:
   367→          version: 10
   368→
   369→      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
   370→        with:
   371→          node-version: '22'
   372→          cache: 'pnpm'
   373→
   374→      - name: Install dependencies

   376→
   377→      - name: Build packages (excluding desktop)
   378→        run: |
   379→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/crypto build
   380→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/core build
   381→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api-client build
   382→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk-core build
   383→          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk build

   387→  cargo-windows:
   388→    name: Cargo Check & Test (Windows)
   389→    needs: [changes, lint]
   390→    if: |
   391→      !failure() && !cancelled() &&
   392→      needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
   393→    runs-on: windows-latest
   394→    steps:
   395→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
   396→
   397→      - name: Install WinFsp
   398→        shell: powershell
   399→        run: |
   400→          $url = "https://github.com/winfsp/winfsp/releases/download/v2.1/winfsp-2.1.25156.msi"
   401→          $out = "winfsp.msi"
   402→          $expectedHash = "073A70E00F77423E34BED98B86E600DEF93393BA5822204FAC57A29324DB9F7A"
   403→          Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -OutFile $out
   404→          $actualHash = (Get-FileHash $out -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
   405→          if ($actualHash -ne $expectedHash) { throw "WinFsp MSI hash mismatch: expected $expectedHash, got $actualHash" }
   406→          Start-Process msiexec.exe -ArgumentList "/i","$out","/qn","INSTALLLEVEL=1000" -Wait -NoNewWindow
   407→
   408→      - name: Create WinFsp MSI placeholder for resource glob
   409→        shell: powershell
   410→        run: New-Item -ItemType File -Force -Path "apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/winfsp-placeholder.msi"
   411→
   412→      - name: Install Rust toolchain
   413→        run: rustup default stable
   414→
   415→      - uses: actions/cache@v4
   416→        with:
   417→          path: |
   418→            ~/.cargo/registry
   419→            ~/.cargo/git
   420→            apps/desktop/src-tauri/target
   421→          key: windows-cargo-${{ hashFiles('apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock') }}
   422→          restore-keys: windows-cargo-
   423→
   424→      - name: Cargo check
   425→        run: cargo check --manifest-path apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml --no-default-features --features winfsp
   426→
   427→      - name: Cargo test
   428→        run: cargo test --manifest-path apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml --no-default-features --features winfsp
   429→
   430→  cargo-macos:
   431→    name: Cargo Check & Test (macOS)
   432→    needs: [changes, lint]
   433→    if: |
   434→      !failure() && !cancelled() &&
   435→      needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
   436→    runs-on: macos-latest
   437→    steps:
   438→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
   439→
   440→      - name: Install FUSE-T
   441→        run: |
   442→          brew install --cask fuse-t
   443→          sudo mkdir -p /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig
   444→          FUSE_T_PC="/Library/Application Support/fuse-t/pkgconfig/fuse-t.pc"
   445→          if [ ! -f "$FUSE_T_PC" ]; then
   446→            FUSE_T_PC=$(find /Library /usr/local /opt/homebrew -name "fuse-t.pc" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
   447→          fi
   448→          if [ -z "$FUSE_T_PC" ] || [ ! -f "$FUSE_T_PC" ]; then
   449→            echo "::error::fuse-t.pc not found after FUSE-T installation"
   450→            exit 1
   451→          fi
   452→          sudo cp "$FUSE_T_PC" /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/fuse.pc
   453→          sudo sed -i '' 's/^Version:.*/Version: 2.9.9/' /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/fuse.pc
   454→
   455→      - name: Install Rust toolchain
   456→        run: rustup default stable
   457→
   458→      - uses: actions/cache@v4
   459→        with:
   460→          path: |
   461→            ~/.cargo/registry
   462→            ~/.cargo/git
   463→            apps/desktop/src-tauri/target
   464→          key: macos-cargo-${{ hashFiles('apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock') }}
   465→          restore-keys: macos-cargo-
   466→
   467→      - name: Cargo check
   468→        working-directory: apps/desktop/src-tauri
   469→        run: cargo check --no-default-features --features fuse
   470→        env:
   471→          PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig
   472→
   473→      - name: Cargo test
   474→        run: cargo test --manifest-path apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml --no-default-features --features fuse
   475→        env:
   476→          PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig

   488→      - name: Install system dependencies

   501→      - name: Install Rust toolchain

   503→
   504→      - uses: actions/cache@v4
   505→        with:
   506→          path: |
   507→            ~/.cargo/registry
   508→            ~/.cargo/git
   509→            apps/desktop/src-tauri/target
   510→          key: linux-cargo-${{ hashFiles('apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock') }}
   511→          restore-keys: linux-cargo-
   512→
   513→      - name: Cargo check
   514→        working-directory: apps/desktop/src-tauri
   515→        run: cargo check --no-default-features --features fuse
   516→
   517→      - name: Install cargo-llvm-cov
   518→        run: |

   520→          cargo install cargo-llvm-cov --locked
   521→
   522→      - name: Cargo test with coverage
   523→        run: >-

   526→          --no-default-features --features fuse
   527→          --lcov --output-path desktop-lcov.info
   528→

   531→        with:

   533→          files: desktop-lcov.info
   534→          flags: desktop
   535→          fail_ci_if_error: false
   536→

> AGENT

Yes, it makes a lot of sense. Here's the overlap:

**CI `test` job already has:**
- Postgres, IPFS, Redis services
- `pnpm install`, all package builds
- API running context (DB env vars, NODE_ENV=test)

**SDK E2E workflow duplicates all of that**, then adds:
- Mock IPNS routing
- API server startup
- The actual SDK E2E test run

The SDK E2E workflow could become a new job in `ci.yml` that `needs: [test]` (or at minimum `needs: [changes, lint, typecheck]`), reusing the same services. The main difference is SDK E2E needs a running API process + mock IPNS routing, while unit tests don't.

**Proposed structure:**

```
ci.yml
├── changes (existing)
├── lint (existing)
├── typecheck (existing)
├── api-spec (existing)
├── migration-check (existing)
├── test (existing - unit tests + coverage)
├── sdk-e2e (NEW - needs: test, same services, starts API + mock-ipns)
└── build (existing - needs all above)
```

**Benefits:**
- Single workflow = one check on the PR (not two separate CI badges)
- Shared `pnpm install` cache hit (same runner image, same lockfile hash)
- `sdk-e2e` only runs after unit tests pass (fail-fast)
- Path filtering via the existing `changes` job

**Trade-off:** Jobs within a workflow can't share service containers — each job spins up its own. So the services are still duplicated at the runner level. But the `pnpm install` and package builds can be cached via artifacts or the pnpm cache.

Want me to go ahead and merge the SDK E2E into `ci.yml`?

> AGENT

Done. The CI pipeline is now:

```
ci.yml (single workflow, one PR check)
├── changes       ─ path detection
├── lint           ─ eslint
├── typecheck      ─ tsc
├── api-spec       ─ verify OpenAPI client in sync
├── migration-check ─ entity/migration drift
├── test           ─ unit tests + coverage (needs: lint, typecheck)
├── sdk-e2e        ─ SDK E2E against live API (needs: test) ← NEW
├── build          ─ production build (needs: all above) 
└── cargo-*        ─ Rust checks (needs: lint, desktop changes)
```

The standalone `sdk-e2e.yml` is deleted. The SDK E2E tests now only run after unit tests pass, and their failure blocks the `Build` check (which is a required status check for merging).
